#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Django's command-line utility for administrative tasks."""
import os
import sys


def main():
    """Run administrative tasks."""
    os.environ.setdefault('DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE', 'core.settings')
    try:
        from django.core.management import execute_from_command_line
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise ImportError(
            "Couldn't import Django. Are you sure it's installed and "
            "available on your PYTHONPATH environment variable? Did you "
            "forget to activate a virtual environment?"
        ) from exc
    execute_from_command_line(sys.argv)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

File: models.py | Lines: 163
Path: backend\academics\models.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from django.db import models
from structure.models import Class
from user.models import Staff,Student,Teacher

# The course model
class Course(models.Model):
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=False,blank=False)
    code = models.CharField(max_length=20,null=False,blank=False)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=150,blank=False,null=False)
    desc = models.TextField(null=False,blank=False)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Course {self.code}"
    
# Course Type model
class CourseType(models.Model):
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=False,blank=False)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=150,blank=False,null=False)
    desc = models.TextField(null=False,blank=False)
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"CourseType: {self.name}"
    

# class has course model
class ClassCourse(models.Model):
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=False,blank=False)
    _class = models.ForeignKey(Class,on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    course = models.ForeignKey(Course,on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    credit = models.IntegerField(null=False,blank=False)
    academic_year = models.CharField(max_length=10,null=False,blank=False)
    semester_num = models.IntegerField(null=False,blank=False)
    course_type = models.ForeignKey(CourseType,on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"Class {self._class.name} offer {self.course.code}"
    
# Teacher's Assignment in a class
class TeacherAssignment(models.Model):
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=False,blank=False)
    teacher = models.ForeignKey(Teacher,on_delete=models.CASCADE,null=False,blank=False)
    class_course = models.OneToOneField(ClassCourse,null=False,blank=False,on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    academic_year = models.CharField(max_length=15,blank=False,null=False)
    semester_num = models.IntegerField(null=False,blank=False)

# Exam class models
class Exam(models.Model):
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=False,blank=False)
    class_course = models.ForeignKey(ClassCourse,on_delete=models.CASCADE,null=True,blank=True)
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"Exam of {self.class_course.course.name} in {self.class_course._class.name}"
   

# Exam types models 
class ExamType(models.Model):
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=False,blank=False)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=45,null=False,blank=False)
   
    def __str__(self):
       return f"ExamType : {self.name}"

# Exam and Their mapped Types
class ExamExamType(models.Model):
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=True,blank=True)
    exam = models.ForeignKey(Exam,on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    exam_type = models.ForeignKey(ExamType,on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    exam_date = models.DateField()
    weight_percent = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5,decimal_places=2,null=False,blank=False)
    max_mark = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5,decimal_places=2,null=True,blank=True)
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"Exam: {self.exam.id} of Type: {self.exam_type.name} on {self.exam_date}"



# Exams Results
class ExamResult(models.Model):
    class Remark(models.TextChoices):
        EXCELLENT = "Excellent"
        VERYGOOD = "Very Good"
        GOOD = "Good"
        AVERAGE = "Average"
        FAIRLYGOOD = "Fairly Good"
        WEAK = "Weak"
    
    
    mark_obtained = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5,decimal_places=2,null=True,blank=True)
    remark = models.CharField(choices=Remark.choices,null=True,blank=True)
    submitted_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    is_published = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    published_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True,blank=True)
    exam_exam_type = models.ForeignKey(ExamExamType,on_delete=models.CASCADE,related_name="exam_results",null=True,blank=True,default=None)
    uploaded_by = models.ForeignKey(Staff,on_delete=models.SET_NULL,null=True,blank=True)
    student = models.ForeignKey(Student,on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"Student: {self.student.matricule} scored: {self.mark_obtained}"
    
    def get_gpa(self):
        remark_to_gpa = {
            self.Remark.EXCELLENT: 4.0,
            self.Remark.VERYGOOD: 3.7,
            self.Remark.GOOD: 3.3,
            self.Remark.FAIRLYGOOD: 3.0,
            self.Remark.AVERAGE: 2.0,
            self.Remark.WEAK: 0.0,
        }
        return remark_to_gpa.get(self.remark,0.0)
    

# Complaints models
class Complaint(models.Model):
    
    # complaints status
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        SUBMITTED = "Submitted"
        APPROVED = "Approved"
        RESPONDED = "Responded"
        SOLVED = "Solved"
        REJECTED = "Rejected"
        REVIEW = "Under Review","under review"
        
    original_mark = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5,decimal_places=2,null=True,blank=True)
    concern = models.TextField(null=False,blank=False)
    evidence = models.ImageField(upload_to="complaints/evidence/",null=True,blank=True)
    resolution_sought = models.CharField(max_length=100,null=False,blank=False)
    student = models.ForeignKey(Student,on_delete=models.CASCADE,blank=False,null=False)
    exam_exam_type = models.ForeignKey(ExamExamType,on_delete=models.CASCADE,blank=False,null=False)
    status = models.CharField(choices=Status.choices,default=Status.SUBMITTED)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True,null=True,blank=True)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"The student: {self.student.matricule} concern: {self.concern}"



# Transcript model
class Transcript(models.Model):
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING   = "Pending"
        VALIDATED = "Validated"
        REJECTED  = "Rejected"

    student       = models.ForeignKey(Student, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="transcripts")
    requested_at  = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    academic_year = models.CharField(max_length=10, null=True, blank=True)
    status        = models.CharField(choices=Status.choices, default=Status.PENDING, max_length=20)

    # Signatures — each role optionally stamps
    director_signature = models.ForeignKey("user.Signature", null=True, blank=True,
                                           on_delete=models.SET_NULL, related_name="director_transcripts")
    hod_signature      = models.ForeignKey("user.Signature", null=True, blank=True,
                                           on_delete=models.SET_NULL, related_name="hod_transcripts")
    staff_signature    = models.ForeignKey("user.Signature", null=True, blank=True,
                                           on_delete=models.SET_NULL, related_name="staff_transcripts")

    validated_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    notes        = models.TextField(null=True, blank=True)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Transcript({self.student.matricule} – {self.status})"

from rest_framework import serializers

class CourseSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = Course
        fields = "__all__"

class CourseTypeSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = CourseType
        fiels = "__all__"
        
class ClassCourseSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = ClassCourse
        fields = "__all__"
        
class ExamSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta: 
        model = Exam
        fields = "__all__"

class ExamTypeSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = ExamType
        fields = "__all__"
        
class ExamExamTypeSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = ExamExamType
        fields = "__all__"
    
class ExamResultSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class_course_id = serializers.IntegerField()
    exam_exam_type_id = serializers.IntegerField()
    resut_file = serializers.FileField()
    
    class Meta:
        model = ExamResult
        exclude = ["student","published_at"]
        
    def validate_result_file(self,value):
        name = value.name.lower()
        
        if not (name.endswith(".csv") or name.endswith(".xlsx") or name.endswith(".xls")):
            raise serializers.ValidationError(
                "Only .csv, .xlsx or .xls files are accepted."
            )
        return value

class ExamResultRetrieveSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    
    class Meta:
        model = ExamResult
        
        fields = "__all__"
 
class ComplaintStaffSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    student = StudentSerializer()
    course = serializers.CharField(
        source="exam_exam_type.exam.class_course.course.name"
    )
    class Meta:
        model = Complaint
        fields = "__all__"
    
    def update(self,instance,validated_data):
        new_status = validated_data.get("status").lower()
        if new_status == "rejected":
            instance.status = Complaint.Status.REJECTED
        elif new_status == "approved":
            instance.status = Complaint.Status.APPROVED
        elif new_status == "solved":
            instance.status = Complaint.Status.SOLVED
        elif new_status == "under review":
            instance.status = Complaint.Status.REVIEW
        elif new_status == "responded":
            instance.status = Complaint.Status.RESPONDED
        
        instance.save()
        
        return instance
class ComplaintSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = Complaint
        fields = "__all__"
        read_only_fields = ["student","created_at","status","exam_exam_type","course"]

from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation

from django.db import transaction
from django.utils import timezone

from structure.models import Department
from user.serializers import StudentSerializer
from utils.utils import compute_remark
from .serializers import *
from rest_framework import permissions,response,viewsets,status
from core.permissions import IsHOD,IsStaff,IsDirector, IsStudent
from .models import *
from rest_framework.views import APIView
from rest_framework.parsers import MultiPartParser,FormParser
from user.models import HOD,Staff, StudentClassCourse

class ClassCourseView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]
    def get(self,request,class_id):
        _class = Class.objects.filter(id=class_id).first()
        class_courses = ClassCourse.objects.filter(_class=_class)
        if not class_courses:
            return response.Response({"data":None,"message":"No courses were found for this class"})
        data = []
        for cc in class_courses:
            # get the teacher assignment for this specific course
            teacher_assigned = TeacherAssignment.objects.filter(class_course=cc).first()
            
            data.append({
                    "course":cc.course.name,
                    "credit": cc.credit,
                    "academic_year": cc.academic_year,
                    "semester_num": cc.semester_num,
                    "course_type": cc.course_type.name,
                    "teacher_assigned":  teacher_assigned.teacher.user.fullname() if teacher_assigned else "No Teacher assigned",
                })
        
        return response.Response({"data":data,"message":"successfully fetched"})

class CoursesView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]
    def get(self,request):
        teacher = request.user.teacher
        
        courses = Course.objects.all()
       
        data = [] 
        for c in courses:

            class_course = ClassCourse.objects.filter(
                course_id=c.id
                ).first()
            if class_course._class.department_level_degree.department == teacher.department:
                data.append({
                    "name": c.name,
                    "code": c.code,
                    "academic_year": class_course.academic_year,
                    "semester_num": class_course.semester_num
                })
        return response.Response(data)

class ExamExamTypeView(APIView):
    
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]
    def get(self,request):
        user = request.user
        print(user)
        if user.user_type == "Admin":
    
            exams = Exam.objects.all()
            data = []
            for exam in exams:
                ee_type = ExamExamType.objects.filter(exam=exam).first()
                
                data.append({
                    "exam_id": exam.id,
                    "exam_type": ee_type.exam_type.name,
                    "exam_date": ee_type.exam_date,
                    "Weight_percent": ee_type.weight_percent,
                    "class": exam.class_course._class.name,
                    "course": exam.class_course.course.name
                })
            return response.Response({"data":data})
        
        if user.user_type == "Teacher":
            teacher = Teacher.objects.filter(user=user).first()
            if teacher.is_hod or teacher.is_staff:
                print("HOD")
                hod = HOD.objects.filter(teacher__user = user).first()
                staff = Staff.objects.filter(teacher__user = user).first()
                if hod:
                    exams = Exam.objects.filter(
                        class_course___class__department_level_degree__department = hod.department
                    )
                elif staff:
                    exams = Exam.objects.filter(
                        class_course___class__department_level_degree__department = staff.department
                    )
                data = []
                for exam in exams:
                    ee_type = ExamExamType.objects.filter(exam=exam).first()
                    
                    if ee_type:
                        data.append({
                            "exam_id": exam.id,
                            "exam_exam_type_id": ee_type.id,
                            "class_course_id": exam.class_course.id,
                            "exam_type": ee_type.exam_type.name,
                            "exam_date": ee_type.exam_date,
                            "Weight_percent": ee_type.weight_percent,
                            "class": exam.class_course._class.name,
                            "class_id": exam.class_course._class.id,
                            "course": exam.class_course.course.name
                        })
                return response.Response({"data":data})
            return response.Response({"data":None,"message":"Please you need to be an Hod or a member of Staff"})
        
        return response.Response({"error":"Error"})
    

# Results Upload View
class ResultUploadView(APIView):
    
    permission_classes = [IsStaff]
    # The parser classes to handle file uploads
    parser_classes = [MultiPartParser,FormParser]
    
    def post(self,request,*args,**kwargs):
        
        # 1. Validate form fields
        serializer = ExamResultSerializer(data=request.data)
        
        if not serializer.is_valid():
            return response.Response(serializer.errors)
        
        data = serializer.validated_data
        
        class_course_id = data["class_course_id"]
        exam_exam_type_id = data["exam_exam_type_id"]
        result_file = data["resut_file"]
        print(f"class course: {class_course_id} , exam exam type {exam_exam_type_id}")
        
        # 2. Fetch ClassCourse & verify Staff's department match
        
        try:
            class_course = ClassCourse.objects.select_related(
                "_class","_class__department_level_degree__department","course"
            ).get(id=class_course_id)
            
        except ClassCourse.DoesNotExist:
            return response.Response(
                {"error": f"ClassCourse with id {class_course_id} not found"}
            )
        
        try: 
            exam_exam_type = ExamExamType.objects.select_related(
                "exam_type"
            ).get(id=exam_exam_type_id)
        
        except ExamExamType.DoesNitExist:
            return response.Response(
                {"error":f"Exam type with id {exam_exam_type_id} not found."},
                status=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT
            )
            
        
        # Verify the ExamExamType actually belongs to this ClassCourse
        try:
            Exam.objects.get(
                examexamtype=exam_exam_type,
                class_course=class_course
            )
            
        except Exam.DoesNotExist:
            return response.Response(
                {"error":"Selected Examtype does not belong to the selected class course."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )
            
        # Staff department check
        try: 
            teacher = request.user.teacher
            
            t_staff = Staff.objects.select_related("department").get(teacher=teacher)
            
            staff = teacher.staff
            t_staff_department = staff.department
        except Exception:
            return response.Response(
                {"error":"Staff profile not found for this user."},
                status=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT
            )
        
        class_department = class_course._class.department_level_degree.department   # Accessing the department through the class_course relationship
        
        if t_staff_department != class_department:
            return response.Response(
                {"error":"You can only upload results for classes in your department"},
                status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN
            )
            
        # 3. Parse file
        
        try:
            file_name = result_file.name.lower()
            import pandas as pd
            if file_name.endswith(".csv"):
                df = pd.read_csv(result_file)
                print(df.head())
            else:
                df = pd.read_excel(result_file,skiprows=8) #Skip the first 2 rows since they contain the wrong info
                
        except Exception as e:
            return response.Response(
                {"error":f"Could not read file: {str(e)}"},
                status=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT
            )
            
        #  Normalize column names (strip spaces, lowercase)
        
        df.columns = [ c.strip().lower().replace(" ","_") for c in df.columns]
        df.rename(columns=lambda col: "mark_obtained" if col.startswith("mark") else col ,inplace=True)
        
        required_cols = {"student_name","mark_obtained"}
        missing = required_cols - set(df.columns)
        print(df.head())
        if missing:
            return response.Response(
                {"error":f"Missing columns in file: {missing}."
                 f"Required columns: student_name, mark_obtained"},
                status=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT
                
            )   
            
        # 4. fetch enrolled students for this ClassDourse
        enrolled_students = StudentClassCourse.objects.filter(
            class_course = class_course,
            status = StudentClassCourse.Status.ENROLLED
        ).select_related("student")
        
        # Build a lookup: full_name (lowercase)
        
        enrolled_map = {
            enr.student.user.fullname().lower(): enr.student
            for enr in enrolled_students
        }
        
        # 5. Update or Create ExamResults
        
        created_count = 0
        updated_count = 0
        skipped_rows = []
        
        try:
            with transaction.atomic():
                for idx,row in df.iterrows():
                    row_num = idx + 2  #human readable row number (1-indexed + header)
                    
                    raw_name = str(row.get("student_name","")).strip().lower()
                    raw_mark = row.get("mark_obtained",None)
                    
                    # Validate mark
                    
                    try:
                        mark = Decimal(str(raw_mark))
                        
                        if not (0 <= mark <= 100):
                            raise ValueError("Mark must be between 0 and 100.")
                    
                    except (InvalidOperation,ValueError,TypeError):
                        skipped_rows.append({
                            "row": row_num,
                            "student_name": raw_name,
                            "reason": f"Invalid mark_obtained: '{raw_mark}' (must be 0-100)"
                        })
                        
                        continue
                    
                    # match student
                    
                    student = enrolled_map.get(raw_name)
                    
                    if not student:
                        skipped_rows.append({
                            "row": row_num,
                            "student_name": raw_name,
                            "reason": "Student not enrolled in this class course."
                        })
                        continue
                    # Fetch the exam_exam_type max mark 
                    exam_max_mark = ExamExamType.objects.filter(id=exam_exam_type_id).first()
                    remark = compute_remark(exam_max_mark.max_mark,mark)
                    
                    # Upsert
                    
                    obj,created = ExamResult.objects.update_or_create(
                        student = student,
                        exam_exam_type = exam_exam_type,
                        defaults = {
                            "mark_obtained": mark,
                            "remark": remark,
                            "uploaded_by": staff,
                            "is_published": False,
                        },
                    )
                
                    if created:
                        created_count += 1
                    else:
                        updated_count += 1
        except Exception as e:
            return response.Response(
                {"error":f"Database error during upload: {str(e)}"},
                status=status.HTTP_304_NOT_MODIFIED
            )
        
        
        return response.Response(
            {
                "message":"Results uploaded successfully.",
                "created": created_count,
                "updated": updated_count,
                "skipped": len(skipped_rows),
                "skipped_details": skipped_rows
            },
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED
        )
        
        
# Student Result Retreive
class StudentResultRetrieveView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsStudent]
    
    def get(self,request,semester_num=None,*args,**kwargs):
        student = request.user.student
        if student:
            data = []
            results = ExamResult.objects.filter(
                student=student,
                exam_exam_type__exam__class_course__semester_num=semester_num,
                is_published=True,
                ).all()
            if results:
                for r in results:
                    data.append({
                        "id":r.id,
                        "remark": r.remark,
                        "mark_obtained": r.mark_obtained,
                        "mark_percent": round(r.mark_obtained / r.exam_exam_type.max_mark * 100,2),
                        "max_mark": r.exam_exam_type.max_mark,
                        "gpa": r.get_gpa(),
                        "exam_exam_type": r.exam_exam_type.exam_type.name,
                        "exam_exam_type_id": r.exam_exam_type.id,
                        "course": r.exam_exam_type.exam.class_course.course.name,
                        "semester_num": semester_num
                        
                        })
                
        return response.Response({"Message":f"Welcome","user":data})


# hod and staff result retreive
class StaffResultRetrieveView(APIView):
    
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]
    
    def get(self,request,semester_num=None,class_id=None,student_id=None,*args,**kwargs):
        user = request.user.teacher.hod if request.user.teacher.is_hod else request.user.teacher.staff
        if user:
            data = []
            
            if student_id:
                student = Student.objects.filter(id=student_id).first()
                if not student:
                    return response.Response({"error":"Student not found"})
                
                results = ExamResult.objects.filter(student=student).all()
                
                for r in results:
                    
                    # Get the course type for the class
                    course_type = r.exam_exam_type.exam.class_course.course_type.name
                    data.append({
                        "student": r.student.user.fullname(),
                        "remark": r.remark,
                        "mark_obtained": round(r.mark_obtained / r.exam_exam_type.max_mark * 100,2),
                        "exam_exam_type": r.exam_exam_type.exam_type.name,
                        "course": r.exam_exam_type.exam.class_course.course.name,
                        "semester_num": r.exam_exam_type.exam.class_course.semester_num,
                        "course_type": course_type,
                    })
                
            
            if semester_num and class_id:
            
                # get courses enrolled in the class in a particular semester
                class_courses = ClassCourse.objects.filter(_class_id=class_id,semester_num=semester_num).all()
                
                # get the students enrolled in those courses and their results
                for cc in class_courses:
                    
                    exam_results = ExamResult.objects.filter(
                        exam_exam_type__exam__class_course = cc
                    ).select_related("student","exam_exam_type__exam__class_course__course")
                    
                    for r in exam_results:
                        data.append({
                            "id":r.id,
                            "student": r.student.user.fullname(),
                            "remark": r.remark,
                            "mark_obtained":  round(r.mark_obtained / r.exam_exam_type.max_mark * 100,2),
                            "exam_exam_type": r.exam_exam_type.exam_type.name,
                            "course": r.exam_exam_type.exam.class_course.course.name,
                            "semester_num": semester_num
                        })
                    
            return response.Response(data)
        
        return response.Response({"error":"Sorry try to login"})

# Department Stats view
class DepartmentStatsView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsHOD | IsStaff]

    def get(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        user = request.user
        teacher = getattr(user, "teacher", None)
        if not teacher:
            return response.Response({"error": "Not authorised."}, status=403)

        hod = HOD.objects.filter(teacher=teacher).first()
        staff = Staff.objects.filter(teacher=teacher).first()
        department = hod.department if hod else (staff.department if staff else None)

        if not department:
            return response.Response({"error": "No department found for this user."}, status=404)

        # All classes in this department
        classes = Class.objects.filter(
            department_level_degree__department=department
        )

        # Total students enrolled in those classes (distinct)
        student_ids = StudentClassCourse.objects.filter(
            class_course___class__in=classes
        ).values_list("student_id", flat=True).distinct()
        total_students = len(list(student_ids))

        # Total teaching staff in department
        total_staff = Staff.objects.filter(department=department).count()
        total_staff += HOD.objects.filter(department=department).count()

        # All published exam results in the department
        all_results = ExamResult.objects.filter(
            exam_exam_type__exam__class_course___class__in=classes,
            is_published=True,
            mark_obtained__isnull=False,
        ).select_related(
            "exam_exam_type__exam__class_course__course",
            "exam_exam_type__exam__class_course",
        )

        total_results = all_results.count()

        # Pass rate — anything not "Weak" is a pass
        passed = all_results.exclude(remark=ExamResult.Remark.WEAK).count()
        pass_rate = round((passed / total_results * 100), 1) if total_results > 0 else 0

        # Average GPA proxy: map remark to grade points

        if total_results > 0:
            gpa_sum = sum(
                r.get_gpa() for r in all_results if r.remark
            )
            average_gpa = round(gpa_sum / total_results, 2)
        else:
            average_gpa = 0.0

        # Course performance: average mark per course
        course_map = {}
        for r in all_results:
            course_name = r.exam_exam_type.exam.class_course.course.name
            if course_name not in course_map:
                course_map[course_name] = {"total": Decimal("0"), "count": 0, "students": set()}
            course_map[course_name]["total"] += r.mark_obtained or Decimal("0")
            course_map[course_name]["count"] += 1
            course_map[course_name]["students"].add(r.student_id)

        course_performance = [
            {
                "name": name[:20],  # truncate long names
                "average": round(float(data["total"] / data["count"]), 1) if data["count"] > 0 else 0,
                "students": len(data["students"]),
            }
            for name, data in course_map.items()
        ]
        # Sort by average descending
        course_performance.sort(key=lambda x: x["average"], reverse=True)

        # Semester trends: average mark per semester
        semester_map = {}
        for r in all_results:
            sem = r.exam_exam_type.exam.class_course.semester_num
            if sem not in semester_map:
                semester_map[sem] = {"total": Decimal("0"), "count": 0}
            semester_map[sem]["total"] += r.mark_obtained or Decimal("0")
            semester_map[sem]["count"] += 1

        semester_trends = [
            {
                "semester": f"Sem {sem}",
                "average": round(float(data["total"] / data["count"]), 1) if data["count"] > 0 else 0,
            }
            for sem, data in sorted(semester_map.items())
        ]

        # Grade distribution from remark field
        remark_to_grade = {
            ExamResult.Remark.EXCELLENT: "A+",
            ExamResult.Remark.VERYGOOD: "A",
            ExamResult.Remark.GOOD: "B",
            ExamResult.Remark.FAIRLYGOOD: "C",
            ExamResult.Remark.AVERAGE: "D",
            ExamResult.Remark.WEAK: "F",
        }
        grade_count = {}
        for r in all_results:
            grade = remark_to_grade.get(r.remark, "N/A") if r.remark else "N/A"
            grade_count[grade] = grade_count.get(grade, 0) + 1

        grade_distribution = [
            {
                "name": grade,
                "value": count,
                "percentage": round(count / total_results * 100, 1) if total_results > 0 else 0,
            }
            for grade, count in sorted(grade_count.items())
        ]

        return response.Response({
            "department": department.name,
            "totalStudents": total_students,
            "totalStaff": total_staff,
            "averageGPA": average_gpa,
            "passRate": pass_rate,
            "coursePerformance": course_performance,
            "semesterTrends": semester_trends,
            "gradeDistribution": grade_distribution,
        })


# Result Publishment view
class ResultPublishmentsView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        """Publish or unpublish results for a given exam_exam_type_id."""
        if hasattr(request.user.teacher,"staff"):
            exam_exam_type_id = request.data.get("exam_exam_type_id")
            action = request.data.get("action")  # "publish" or "unpublish"

            if not exam_exam_type_id or action not in ("publish", "unpublish"):
                return response.Response(
                    {"error": "Provide exam_exam_type_id and action ('publish' or 'unpublish')."},
                    status=400,
                )

            exam_exam_type = ExamExamType.objects.filter(id=exam_exam_type_id).first()
            if not exam_exam_type:
                return response.Response({"error": "ExamExamType not found."}, status=404)

            is_published = action == "publish"
            published_at = timezone.now() if is_published else None

            updated = ExamResult.objects.filter(exam_exam_type=exam_exam_type).update(
                is_published=is_published,
                published_at=published_at,
            )

            return response.Response(
                {
                    "message": f"Successfully {'published' if is_published else 'unpublished'} {updated} result(s).",
                    "exam_exam_type_id": exam_exam_type_id,
                    "is_published": is_published,
                    "count": updated,
                },
                status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED
            )
        
        return response.Response({"error":"You don't have the permission to publish results"},status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

    def get(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        """Return publish status summary for all exam_exam_types in the user's department."""
        user = request.user
        teacher = getattr(user, "teacher", None)
        if not teacher:
            return response.Response({"error": "Not authorised."}, status=403)

        hod = HOD.objects.filter(teacher=teacher).first()
        staff = Staff.objects.filter(teacher=teacher).first()
        department = hod.department if hod else (staff.department if staff else None)

        exams = Exam.objects.filter(
            class_course___class__department_level_degree__department=department
        )

        data = []
        for exam in exams:
            for eet in ExamExamType.objects.filter(exam=exam):
                total = ExamResult.objects.filter(exam_exam_type=eet).count()
                published = ExamResult.objects.filter(exam_exam_type=eet, is_published=True).count()
                data.append({
                    "exam_exam_type_id": eet.id,
                    "course": exam.class_course.course.name,
                    "class": exam.class_course._class.name,
                    "exam_type": eet.exam_type.name,
                    "exam_date": eet.exam_date,
                    "total_results": total,
                    "published_results": published,
                    "is_fully_published": total > 0 and total == published,
                })

        return response.Response({"data": data})

# complaint view
class ComplaintViewset(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]
    # for getting the diverse serializer classes
    def get_serializer_class(self):
        if hasattr(self.request.user,"teacher"):
            return ComplaintStaffSerializer
        
        if hasattr(self.request.user,"student"):
            return ComplaintSerializer
    
    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        
        if hasattr(user,"teacher"):
            return Complaint.objects.filter(
                exam_exam_type__exam__class_course___class__department_level_degree__department = user.teacher.department
            ).order_by("-created_at").all()
        
        return Complaint.objects.filter(student=user.student)
    
    def perform_create(self,serializer):
        
        student = self.request.user.student
        
        exam_exam_type_id = self.request.data.get("exam_exam_type_id")
        
        # get the exact exam_exam_type
        exam_exam_type = ExamExamType.objects.filter(id=exam_exam_type_id).first()
        
        if not exam_exam_type:
            raise serializers.ValidationError({"error":"Exam exam type not found."})
        
        complaint = serializer.save(
            exam_exam_type=exam_exam_type,
            student=student,
            concern=self.request.data.get("concern"),
            evidence=self.request.FILES.get("evidence"),
            status = Complaint.Status.SUBMITTED
        )
        
class TranscriptView(APIView):
    """Student requests a transcript; staff/HOD/director validate it."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        user = request.user

        # Student: fetch own transcripts
        if user.user_type == "Student":
            student = getattr(user, "student", None)
            if not student:
                return response.Response({"error": "Student not found."}, status=404)
            transcripts = Transcript.objects.filter(student=student).order_by("-requested_at")
        else:
            # Director sees all; HOD/Staff sees their dept
            teacher = getattr(user, "teacher", None)
            if user.user_type == "Admin":
                # get all the departments in the school
                director = getattr(user,"director")
                school = director.school if director else None
                
                if not school:
                    return response.Response({"error":"No schools assigned to this director"})
                 
                departments = Department.objects.filter(school=school)
                
                # get transcripts of students enrolled in courses within those department
                transcripts = Transcript.objects.filter(
                    student__studentclasscourse__class_course___class__department_level_degree__department__in=departments
                ).distinct().order_by("-requested_at")
            elif teacher:
                dept = teacher.department
                transcripts = Transcript.objects.filter(
                    student__studentclasscourse__class_course___class__department_level_degree__department=dept
                ).distinct().order_by("-requested_at")
            else:
                return response.Response({"error": "Not authorised."}, status=403)

        data = []
        for t in transcripts:
            # Gather published results for the student
            results_qs = ExamResult.objects.filter(
                student=t.student, is_published=True
            ).select_related(
                "exam_exam_type__exam__class_course__course",
                "exam_exam_type__exam__class_course",
                "exam_exam_type__exam_type",
            )
            results = []
            for r in results_qs:
                cc = r.exam_exam_type.exam.class_course
                results.append({
                    "course":        cc.course.name,
                    "course_code":   cc.course.code,
                    "credits":       cc.credit,
                    "semester":      cc.semester_num,
                    "academic_year": cc.academic_year,
                    "exam_type":     r.exam_exam_type.exam_type.name,
                    "mark":          str(r.mark_obtained),
                    "remark":        r.remark,
                })

            data.append({
                "id":                   t.id,
                "student_name":         t.student.user.fullname(),
                "student_matricule":    t.student.matricule,
                "student_email":        t.student.school_mail,
                "academic_year":        t.academic_year,
                "status":               t.status,
                "requested_at":         t.requested_at,
                "validated_at":         t.validated_at,
                "notes":                t.notes,
                "has_director_sig":     t.director_signature_id is not None,
                "has_hod_sig":          t.hod_signature_id is not None,
                "has_staff_sig":        t.staff_signature_id is not None,
                "director_sig_url":     request.build_absolute_uri(t.director_signature.image.url) if t.director_signature else None,
                "hod_sig_url":          request.build_absolute_uri(t.hod_signature.image.url) if t.hod_signature else None,
                "staff_sig_url":        request.build_absolute_uri(t.staff_signature.image.url) if t.staff_signature else None,
                "results":              results,
            })
        return response.Response(data)

    def post(self, request):
        """Student creates a transcript request."""
        user = request.user
        if user.user_type != "Student":
            return response.Response({"error": "Only students can request transcripts."}, status=403)
        student = getattr(user, "student", None)
        if not student:
            return response.Response({"error": "Student profile not found."}, status=404)

        academic_year = request.data.get("academic_year", "")
        # Prevent duplicate pending requests
        existing = Transcript.objects.filter(student=student, status=Transcript.Status.PENDING).first()
        if existing:
            return response.Response({"error": "You already have a pending transcript request."}, status=400)

        transcript = Transcript.objects.create(student=student, academic_year=academic_year)
        return response.Response({"message": "Transcript request submitted.", "id": transcript.id}, status=201)


class TranscriptValidateView(APIView):
    """Director/HOD/Staff signs a transcript with their stored signature."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, transcript_id):
        user = request.user
        transcript = Transcript.objects.filter(id=transcript_id).first()
        if not transcript:
            return response.Response({"error": "Transcript not found."}, status=404)

        action = request.data.get("action", "validate")  # validate | reject

        if action == "reject":
            transcript.status = Transcript.Status.REJECTED
            transcript.notes = request.data.get("notes", "")
            transcript.validated_at = timezone.now()
            transcript.save()
            return response.Response({"message": "Transcript rejected."})

        # Get signer's signature
        sig = None
        role = None
        if user.user_type == "Admin" and hasattr(user, "director"):
            sig = user.director.signature
            role = "director"
        elif user.user_type == "Teacher" and hasattr(user, "teacher"):
            if user.teacher.is_hod and hasattr(user.teacher, "hod"):
                sig = user.teacher.hod.signature
                role = "hod"
            elif user.teacher.is_staff and hasattr(user.teacher, "staff"):
                sig = user.teacher.staff.signature
                role = "staff"

        if not sig:
            return response.Response(
                {"error": "You have no signature on file. Please upload your signature in your profile first."},
                status=400,
            )

        # Attach signature
        if role == "director":
            transcript.director_signature = sig
        elif role == "hod":
            transcript.hod_signature = sig
        elif role == "staff":
            transcript.staff_signature = sig

        transcript.status = Transcript.Status.VALIDATED
        transcript.validated_at = timezone.now()
        transcript.notes = request.data.get("notes", transcript.notes)
        transcript.save()

        return response.Response({"message": f"Transcript validated by {role}.", "status": transcript.status})


class ClassRankingView(APIView):
    """Returns class ranking + averages for a semester (student sees own rank)."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request, semester_num):
        user = request.user
        student = getattr(user, "student", None)
        if not student:
            return response.Response({"error": "Student not found."}, status=404)

        # Find the class the student is enrolled in for this semester
        enrollment = StudentClassCourse.objects.filter(
            student=student,
            class_course__semester_num=semester_num,
        ).first()
        if not enrollment:
            return response.Response({"data": [], "my_rank": None, "class_average": None})

        target_class = enrollment.class_course._class

        # All students in that class this semester
        enrollments = StudentClassCourse.objects.filter(
            class_course___class=target_class,
            class_course__semester_num=semester_num,
        ).values_list("student", flat=True).distinct()

        student_averages = []
        for sid in enrollments:
            results = ExamResult.objects.filter(
                student_id=sid,
                exam_exam_type__exam__class_course__semester_num=semester_num,
                exam_exam_type__exam__class_course___class=target_class,
                is_published=True,
                mark_obtained__isnull=False,
            )
            count = results.count()
            if count == 0:
                continue
            total = sum(float( r.mark_obtained / r.exam_exam_type.max_mark * 100) for r in results)
            avg = round(total / count, 2)
            s = Student.objects.get(id=sid)
            student_averages.append({
                "student_id":   sid,
                "name":         s.user.fullname(),
                "matricule":    s.matricule,
                "average":      avg,
                "is_me":        sid == student.id,
            })

        # Sort by average descending
        student_averages.sort(key=lambda x: x["average"], reverse=True)
        for i, s in enumerate(student_averages):
            s["rank"] = i + 1

        class_avg = round(
            sum(s["average"] for s in student_averages) / len(student_averages), 2
        ) if student_averages else None

        my_rank = next((s["rank"] for s in student_averages if s["is_me"]), None)

        return response.Response({
            "class_name":    target_class.name,
            "semester":      semester_num,
            "class_average": class_avg,
            "my_rank":       my_rank,
            "total_students": len(student_averages),
            "ranking":       student_averages,
        })

from django.contrib import admin

class AnnouncementAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('title', 'sender', 'target_type', 'created_at')
    list_filter = ('target_type', 'created_at')
    search_fields = ('title', 'content', 'sender__username')

@admin.register(AnnouncementFile)
class AnnouncementFileAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('announcement', 'file', 'uploaded_at')

@admin.register(Timetable)
class TimetableAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('title', 'department', 'uploaded_by', 'uploaded_at')
    list_filter = ('department', 'uploaded_at')

@admin.register(TimetableClass)
class TimetableClassAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('timetable', 'get_class_name')

        return obj._class.name
    get_class_name.short_description = 'Class'

# custom middleware for x-frame-options
class AllowIframeMiddleware:
    def __init__(self,get_response):
        self.get_response = get_response
    
    def __call__(self,request):
        response = self.get_response(request)
        if 'x-frame-options' in response.headers:
            del response.headers['x-frame-options']
        return response

------------------------------------------------------------
File: models.py | Lines: 52
Path: backend\announcements\models.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from structure.models import Department, Class
from user.models import Student

User = get_user_model()

class Announcement(models.Model):
    class TargetType(models.TextChoices):
        CLASS = 'class', 'Class'
        DEPARTMENT = 'department', 'Department'
        UNIVERSITY = 'school', 'School'

    title = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    content = models.TextField()
    sender = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    target_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=TargetType.choices)
    target_class = models.ForeignKey(Class, on_delete=models.CASCADE, null=True, blank=True)
    target_department = models.ForeignKey(Department, on_delete=models.CASCADE, null=True, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.title} - {self.sender.username}"

class AnnouncementFile(models.Model):
    announcement = models.ForeignKey(Announcement, related_name='files', on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    file = models.FileField(upload_to='announcements/files/')
    uploaded_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"File for {self.announcement.title}"

class Timetable(models.Model):
    pdf_file = models.FileField(upload_to='timetables/')
    department = models.ForeignKey(Department, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    uploaded_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    uploaded_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    title = models.CharField(max_length=200, default='Timetable')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Timetable for {self.department.name} by {self.uploaded_by.username}"

class TimetableClass(models.Model):
    timetable = models.ForeignKey(Timetable, related_name='classes', on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    _class = models.ForeignKey(Class, on_delete=models.CASCADE)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ('timetable', '_class')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.timetable.title} - {self._class.name}"

File: serializers.py | Lines: 78
Path: backend\announcements\serializers.py

from rest_framework import serializers
from .models import Announcement, AnnouncementFile, Timetable, TimetableClass
from academics.models import ExamResult

class AnnouncementFileSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = AnnouncementFile
        fields = ['id', 'file', 'uploaded_at']

class AnnouncementSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    files = AnnouncementFileSerializer(many=True, read_only=True)
    uploaded_files = serializers.ListField(
        child=serializers.FileField(),
        write_only=True,
        required=False
    )
    sender_name = serializers.CharField(source='sender.get_full_name', read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = Announcement
        fields = ['id', 'title', 'content', 'sender', 'sender_name', 'target_type', 
                 'target_class', 'target_department', 'created_at', 'updated_at', 'files', 'uploaded_files']
        read_only_fields = ['sender', 'created_at', 'updated_at']

    def create(self, validated_data):
        
        uploaded_files = validated_data.pop('uploaded_files', [])
        announcement = super().create(validated_data)
        
        for file in uploaded_files:
            AnnouncementFile.objects.create(announcement=announcement, file=file)
        
        return announcement

class TimetableClassSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class_name = serializers.CharField(source='_class.name', read_only=True)
    
    class Meta:
        model = TimetableClass
        fields = ['id', '_class', 'class_name']

class TimetableSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    classes = TimetableClassSerializer(many=True, read_only=True)
    class_ids = serializers.ListField(
        child=serializers.IntegerField(),
        write_only=True,
        required=False
    )
    department_name = serializers.CharField(source='department.name', read_only=True)
    uploaded_by_name = serializers.CharField(source='uploaded_by.get_full_name', read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = Timetable
        fields = ['id', 'pdf_file', 'department', 'department_name', 'uploaded_by', 
                 'uploaded_by_name', 'uploaded_at', 'title', 'classes', 'class_ids']
        read_only_fields = ['uploaded_by', 'uploaded_at','department']

    def create(self, validated_data):
        class_ids = validated_data.pop('class_ids', [])
        timetable = super().create(validated_data)
        
        for class_id in class_ids:
            TimetableClass.objects.create(timetable=timetable, _class_id=class_id)
        
        return timetable

class StudentResultSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    course_name = serializers.CharField(source='exam_exam_type.exam.class_course.course.name', read_only=True)
    course_code = serializers.CharField(source='exam_exam_type.exam.class_course.course.code', read_only=True)
    class_name = serializers.CharField(source='exam_exam_type.exam.class_course._class.name', read_only=True)
    exam_type = serializers.CharField(source='exam_exam_type.exam_type.name', read_only=True)
    semester = serializers.IntegerField(source='exam_exam_type.exam.class_course.semester_num', read_only=True)
    academic_year = serializers.CharField(source='exam_exam_type.exam.class_course.academic_year', read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = ExamResult
        fields = ['id', 'course_name', 'course_code', 'class_name', 'exam_type', 
                 'mark_obtained', 'remark', 'submitted_at', 'is_published', 'semester', 'academic_year']

File: views.py | Lines: 128
Path: backend\announcements\views.py

from structure.models import Department
from .models import Announcement, Timetable
from .serializers import AnnouncementSerializer, TimetableSerializer
from rest_framework import viewsets, serializers
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated
from django.db.models import Q
from academics.models import ExamResult
from user.models import Student


class AnnouncementViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = AnnouncementSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    queryset = Announcement.objects.all()

    def _director_school_departments(self, user):
        """Return departments that belong to the director's school."""
        director = getattr(user, "director", None)
        if not director or not director.school:
            return Department.objects.none()
        return Department.objects.filter(school=director.school)

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user

        if hasattr(user, "director"):
            # Director sees only announcements in his school
            school_depts = self._director_school_departments(user)
            return Announcement.objects.filter(
                Q(target_type=Announcement.TargetType.UNIVERSITY) |
                Q(target_type=Announcement.TargetType.DEPARTMENT, target_department__in=school_depts) |
                Q(target_type=Announcement.TargetType.CLASS,
                  target_class__department_level_degree__department__in=school_depts)
            ).order_by("-created_at")

        elif hasattr(user, "teacher"):
            return Announcement.objects.filter(
                Q(target_type=Announcement.TargetType.UNIVERSITY) |
                Q(target_type=Announcement.TargetType.DEPARTMENT, target_department=user.teacher.department)
            ).order_by("-created_at")

        elif hasattr(user, "student"):
            student_classes = user.student.studentclasscourse_set.values_list(
                "class_course___class", flat=True
            )
            student_departments = Department.objects.filter(
                departmentleveldegree__class__in=student_classes
            ).distinct()
            return Announcement.objects.filter(
                Q(target_type=Announcement.TargetType.UNIVERSITY) |
                Q(target_type=Announcement.TargetType.DEPARTMENT, target_department__in=student_departments) |
                Q(target_type=Announcement.TargetType.CLASS, target_class__in=student_classes)
            ).order_by("-created_at")

        return Announcement.objects.none()

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        user        = self.request.user
        target_type = serializer.validated_data.get("target_type")

        if hasattr(user, "director"):
            school_depts = self._director_school_departments(user)

            # Director can only target departments/classes in his school
            if target_type == Announcement.TargetType.DEPARTMENT:
                target_dept = serializer.validated_data.get("target_department")
                if target_dept and target_dept not in school_depts:
                    raise serializers.ValidationError(
                        "You can only send announcements to departments in your school."
                    )
            elif target_type == Announcement.TargetType.CLASS:
                target_class = serializer.validated_data.get("target_class")
                if target_class and target_class.department_level_degree.department not in school_depts:
                    raise serializers.ValidationError(
                        "You can only send announcements to classes in your school."
                    )
            # UNIVERSITY type is fine for directors
            serializer.save(sender=user)
            return

        elif hasattr(user, "teacher"):
            if target_type == Announcement.TargetType.UNIVERSITY:
                raise serializers.ValidationError("Only directors can send university-wide announcements.")
            if target_type == Announcement.TargetType.DEPARTMENT:
                target_dept = serializer.validated_data.get("target_department")
                if target_dept != user.teacher.department:
                    raise serializers.ValidationError("You can only send announcements to your department.")
            elif target_type == Announcement.TargetType.CLASS:
                target_class = serializer.validated_data.get("target_class")
                if target_class.department_level_degree.department != user.teacher.department:
                    raise serializers.ValidationError("You can only send announcements to classes in your department.")
            serializer.save(sender=user)
            return

        raise serializers.ValidationError("You don't have permission to send announcements.")


class TimetableViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = TimetableSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    queryset = Timetable.objects.all()

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user

        if hasattr(user, "director"):
            director = user.director
            if director.school:
                school_depts = Department.objects.filter(school=director.school)
                return Timetable.objects.filter(department__in=school_depts).distinct()
            return Timetable.objects.none()

        elif hasattr(user, "student"):
            student_classes = user.student.studentclasscourse_set.values_list(
                "class_course___class", flat=True
            )
            return Timetable.objects.filter(classes___class__in=student_classes).distinct()

        elif hasattr(user, "teacher"):
            return Timetable.objects.filter(department=user.teacher.department)

        return Timetable.objects.none()

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        user = self.request.user
        if not hasattr(user, "teacher"):
            raise serializers.ValidationError("Only teachers can upload timetables.")
        serializer.save(uploaded_by=user, department=user.teacher.department)

from django.core.files.storage import FileSystemStorage
import os

class OverWriteStorage(FileSystemStorage):
    def get_available_name(self, name, max_length=None):
        if self.exists(name):
            os.remove(os.path.join(self.location, name))
            print("By master Franck")
        return name

File: permissions.py | Lines: 27
Path: backend\core\permissions.py

from rest_framework.permissions import BasePermission
from user.models import User

class IsDirector(BasePermission):
    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        return request.user.is_authenticated and request.user.user_type == User.UserType.DIRECTOR
    
class IsTeacher(BasePermission):
    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        return request.user.is_authenticated and request.user.user_type == User.UserType.TEACHER
    
class IsHOD(BasePermission):
    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        return (request.user.is_authenticated)\
            and (request.user.user_type == User.UserType.TEACHER)\
            and (request.user.teacher.is_hod == True)
            
class IsStaff(BasePermission):
    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        return (request.user.is_authenticated)\
            and (request.user.user_type == User.UserType.TEACHER)\
            and (request.user.teacher.is_staff == True)

class IsStudent(BasePermission):
    def has_permission(self,request,view):
        return request.user.is_authenticated and request.user.user_type == User.UserType.STUDENT

File: views.py | Lines: 198
Path: backend\dashboards\views.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from django.shortcuts import render
from rest_framework.decorators import api_view,permission_classes
from rest_framework.response import Response

from core.permissions import *
from structure.models import *
from academics.models import ExamResult, Complaint, ClassCourse, Transcript
from user.models import Student, HOD, Staff, StudentClassCourse

# The various roles dashboards
# Director's Dashboard
@api_view(["GET"])
@permission_classes([IsDirector])
def director_dashboard(request):
    director = getattr(request.user, "director", None)
    school   = director.school if director else None

    if not school:
        return Response({"error": "No school assigned to this director."}, status=400)

    # All departments in this school
    departments = Department.objects.filter(school=school)

    # All classes in those departments
    classes = Class.objects.filter(
        department_level_degree__department__in=departments
    )

    # Students enrolled in those classes
    student_ids = StudentClassCourse.objects.filter(
        class_course___class__in=classes
    ).values_list("student_id", flat=True).distinct()
    total_students = len(list(student_ids))

    # Pending complaints in this school
    pending_complaints = Complaint.objects.filter(
        exam_exam_type__exam__class_course___class__in=classes,
        status=Complaint.Status.SUBMITTED,
    ).count()

    # Pending transcript requests in this school
    pending_transcripts = Transcript.objects.filter(
        student__studentclasscourse__class_course___class__in=classes,
        status=Transcript.Status.PENDING,
    ).distinct().count()

    # Unpublished results in this school
    unpublished_results = ExamResult.objects.filter(
        exam_exam_type__exam__class_course___class__in=classes,
        is_published=False,
    ).count()

    # Per-department breakdown
    dept_breakdown = []
    for dept in departments:
        dept_classes = classes.filter(department_level_degree__department=dept)
        dept_students = StudentClassCourse.objects.filter(
            class_course___class__in=dept_classes
        ).values_list("student_id", flat=True).distinct()
        dept_breakdown.append({
            "id":       dept.id,
            "name":     dept.name,
            "code":     dept.code,
            "logo":     request.build_absolute_uri(dept.logo.url) if dept.logo else None,
            "classes":  dept_classes.count(),
            "students": len(list(dept_students)),
        })

    data = {
        "school_name":          school.name,
        "school_code":          school.code,
        "school_logo":          request.build_absolute_uri(school.logo.url) if school.logo else None,
        "total_departments":    departments.count(),
        "total_classes":        classes.count(),
        "total_students":       total_students,
        "pending_complaints":   pending_complaints,
        "pending_transcripts":  pending_transcripts,
        "unpublished_results":  unpublished_results,
        "departments":          dept_breakdown,
    }
    return Response(data)


# Hod's Dashboard
@api_view(["GET"])
@permission_classes([IsHOD])
def hod_dashboard(request):
    teacher = request.user.teacher
    hod = HOD.objects.filter(teacher=teacher).first()
    department = hod.department if hod else None

    # Classes in this HOD's department
    classes = Class.objects.filter(
        department_level_degree__department=department
    )
    total_classes = classes.count()

    # Students enrolled in those classes
    student_ids = StudentClassCourse.objects.filter(
        class_course___class__in=classes
    ).values_list("student_id", flat=True).distinct()
    total_students = len(student_ids)

    # Pending complaints
    pending_complaints = Complaint.objects.filter(
        exam_exam_type__exam__class_course___class__in=classes,
        status=Complaint.Status.SUBMITTED,
    ).count()

    # Unpublished results
    unpublished_results = ExamResult.objects.filter(
        exam_exam_type__exam__class_course___class__in=classes,
        is_published=False,
    ).count()

    data = {
        "department": department.name if department else "N/A",
        "total_classes": total_classes,
        "total_students": total_students,
        "pending_complaints": pending_complaints,
        "unpublished_results": unpublished_results,
    }
    
    return Response(data)
    

# Staff's Dashboard
@api_view(["GET"])
@permission_classes([IsStaff])
def staff_dashboard(request):
    teacher = request.user.teacher
    staff = Staff.objects.filter(teacher=teacher).first()
    department = staff.department if staff else None

    classes = Class.objects.filter(
        department_level_degree__department=department
    )

    student_ids = StudentClassCourse.objects.filter(
        class_course___class__in=classes
    ).values_list("student_id", flat=True).distinct()

    pending_complaints = Complaint.objects.filter(
        exam_exam_type__exam__class_course___class__in=classes,
        status=Complaint.Status.SUBMITTED,
    ).count()

    data = {
        "department": department.name if department else "N/A",
        "total_classes": classes.count(),
        "total_students": len(student_ids),
        "pending_complaints": pending_complaints,
    }
    return Response(data)


# Student's Dashboard
@api_view(["GET"])
@permission_classes([IsStudent])
def student_dashboard(request):
    student = request.user.student

    # All enrolled courses
    enrollments = StudentClassCourse.objects.filter(
        student=student
    ).select_related("class_course__course", "class_course___class")

    courses = [
        {
            "course_name": e.class_course.course.name,
            "course_code": e.class_course.course.code,
            "class_name": e.class_course._class.name,
            "credits": e.class_course.credit,
            "semester": e.class_course.semester_num,
            "status": e.status,
        }
        for e in enrollments
    ]

    # Published results count
    total_published = ExamResult.objects.filter(
        student=student, is_published=True
    ).count()

    # Pending complaints
    pending_complaints = Complaint.objects.filter(
        student=student, status=Complaint.Status.SUBMITTED
    ).count()

    data = {
        "total_courses": len(courses),
        "total_results_published": total_published,
        "pending_complaints": pending_complaints,
        "courses": courses,
    }
    return Response(data)

from django.db import models
from core.image_write import OverWriteStorage

class School(models.Model):
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=True,blank=True)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=75,null=False,blank=False)
    code = models.CharField(max_length=45,null=False,blank=False)
    logo = models.ImageField(upload_to="school/logo",storage=OverWriteStorage())
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"Code: {self.code} ,Name: {self.name}"


class Department(models.Model):
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=True,blank=True)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=75,null=False,blank=False)
    code = models.CharField(max_length=45,null=False,blank=False)
    logo = models.ImageField(upload_to="department/logo",storage=OverWriteStorage())
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    school = models.ForeignKey(School,on_delete=models.CASCADE,null=False,related_name="departments")
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"Code: {self.code} ,Name: {self.name}"

class LevelDegree(models.Model):
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=True,blank=True)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=75,null=False,blank=False)
    code = models.CharField(max_length=45,null=False,blank=False)
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"Code: {self.code} ,Name: {self.name}"

class DepartmentLevelDegree(models.Model):
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=True,blank=True)
    department = models.ForeignKey(Department,on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    level_degree = models.ForeignKey(LevelDegree,on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    num_levels = models.IntegerField(null=False,blank=False)
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"Dpt: {self.department.name}: Level/Degree: {self.level_degree.name}"
    

class Class(models.Model):
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=True,blank=True)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=75,null=False,blank=False)
    level = models.IntegerField(null=False,blank=False)
    department_level_degree = models.ForeignKey(DepartmentLevelDegree,on_delete=models.SET_NULL,null=True,blank=True)
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"Name: {self.name}"

from rest_framework import serializers

# import all the models of the structure
from .models import *


class SchoolSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    
    logo = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    class Meta:
        model = School
        exclude = ["external_id"]
    
    def get_logo(self, obj):
    
        request = self.context.get("request")
        if obj.logo and hasattr(obj.logo, "url"):
            return request.build_absolute_uri(obj.logo.url)
        return None
    

class DepartmentSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = Department
        exclude = ["external_id"]
        

class LevelDegreeSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = LevelDegree
        exclude = ["external_id"]
        

class DepartmentLevelDegreeSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = DepartmentLevelDegree
        exclude = ["external_id"]

class ClassSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = Class
        exclude = ["external_id"]

from django.urls import path,include
from rest_framework.routers import DefaultRouter
from structure.views import *


router = DefaultRouter()

router.register("schools",SchoolViewset,basename="school")
router.register("departments",DepartmentViewset,basename="department")
router.register("level-degrees",LevelDegreeViewset,basename="level-degree")

urlpatterns = [
    path("department/<int:department_id>/level-degrees/",DepartmentLevelDegreeView.as_view()),
    path("classes/",ClassView.as_view()),
    path("class/<int:id>",ClassView.as_view()),
    path("",include(router.urls))
]

from rest_framework import viewsets,permissions,response
from rest_framework.views import APIView 

from .models import *
from core.permissions import IsDirector,IsHOD
from .serializers import *
from user.models import *



class SchoolViewset(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
   
    serializer_class = SchoolSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsDirector]
    
    def get_queryset(self):
        director = getattr(self.request.user, 'director', None)
        if director and director.school:
            return School.objects.filter(id=director.school.id)
        return School.objects.none()
    
    def get_serializer_context(self):
        context = super().get_serializer_context()
        context.update({"request": self.request})
        return context


class DepartmentViewset(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    
    serializer_class = DepartmentSerializer
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]
    
    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        if hasattr(user, 'director'):
            director = user.director
            if director and director.school:
                return Department.objects.filter(school=director.school).order_by("-created_at")
            return Department.objects.none()
        # HOD/Staff/Teachers can also read departments
        return Department.objects.all().order_by("-created_at")
    
    
class LevelDegreeViewset(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = LevelDegreeSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsDirector]
    queryset = LevelDegree.objects.all()
    
    
    
    
class DepartmentLevelDegreeView(APIView):
    
    serializer_class = DepartmentLevelDegreeSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsDirector]
    
    def get(self,request,department_id):
        department = Department.objects.filter(id=department_id).first()
        department_level_degrees = DepartmentLevelDegree.objects.filter(department=department)
        
        data = []
        for d_l_d in department_level_degrees:
            data.append({
                "id":d_l_d.id,
                "level_degree": d_l_d.level_degree.name,
                "num_levels": d_l_d.num_levels
            })
        
        return response.Response(data)
            
        
    

class ClassView(APIView):
    
    serializer_class = ClassSerializer
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]
    
    
    def get(self,request,id=None):
        user = request.user
        
        if id:
            try:
                _class = Class.objects.get(id=id)
                count_students = StudentClassCourse.objects.filter(class_course___class=_class).values("student").distinct().count()
                level_degree = DepartmentLevelDegree.objects.filter(id=_class.department_level_degree_id).first()
                data = {
                    "id":_class.id,
                    "name":_class.name,
                    "level":_class.level,
                    "level_degree": level_degree.level_degree.name,
                    "num_students": count_students,
                }
                return response.Response(data)
            except Class.DoesNotExist:
                return response.Response({"message":"Class not found"})
            
        if user.user_type == "Admin":
            # get the schools in department
            school = user.director.school
            department = request.query_params.get('department')
            
            if department:
                department = Department.objects.filter(id=department).first()
                print(f"Department :{department}")
                classes = Class.objects.filter(
                department_level_degree__department__id=department.id
                )
                data = [{
                'id': c.id,
                'name': c.name,
                 }
                for c in classes]
            else:
                departments = Department.objects.filter(school=school)
                
                classes = Class.objects.filter(
                    department_level_degree__department__in=departments
                )
                data = [{
                    'id': c.id,
                    'name': c.name,
                    
                }
                for c in classes]
            return response.Response(data)
        
        teacher = Teacher.objects.filter(user=user).first()
        hod = HOD.objects.filter(teacher=teacher).first()
        staff = Staff.objects.filter(teacher=teacher).first()
        
        if hod or staff:
            classes = Class.objects.filter(
                department_level_degree__department=hod.department
            ) if hod else Class.objects.filter(
                department_level_degree__department=staff.department
            )
            data = []
            for _class in classes:
                level_degree = DepartmentLevelDegree.objects.filter(id=_class.department_level_degree_id).first()
                count_students = StudentClassCourse.objects.filter(class_course___class=_class).values("student").distinct().count()
                data.append({
                    "id":_class.id,
                    "name":_class.name,
                    "level":_class.level,
                    "level_degree": level_degree.level_degree.name,
                    "num_students": count_students,
                })
            
            
            return response.Response(data)

File: checks.py | Lines: 19

from .fetch import FetchData


class TeacherRole:
    def __init__(self):
        self.fetch_data = FetchData()
    def isHod(self,teacher:int):
        Teacher = self.fetch_data.fetch_hod_by_teacher_id(teacher)
        if Teacher:
            id,teach_id,department_id = Teacher
            return teach_id == teacher
        return False
    def isStaff(self,teacher:int):
        Teacher = self.fetch_data.fetch_staff_by_teacher_id(teacher)
        if Teacher and not self.isHod(teacher):
            id,teach_id,department_id = Teacher
            return teach_id == teacher
        return False

File: fetch.py | Lines: 110
