from django.db import connections

class FetchData:
    def __init__(self):
        self.cursor = connections['university_db'].cursor()
    
    # Users fetches 
    def fetch_user(self):
        self.cursor.execute("SELECT id,fullname,email,phone,gender,dob,user_type FROM users")
        univ_users = self.cursor.fetchall()
        return univ_users
    
    def fetch_director_by_id(self,id:int):
        self.cursor.execute(f"SELECT matricule,admin_id,school_id,profile_pic FROM admins WHERE admin_id ={id}")
        director = self.cursor.fetchone()
        return director
    def fetch_teacher_by_id(self,id:int):
        self.cursor.execute(f"SELECT matricule,teacher_id,school_email,department_id,profile_pic FROM teachers WHERE teacher_id ={id}")
        teacher = self.cursor.fetchone()
        return teacher
    def fetch_student_by_id(self,id:int):
        self.cursor.execute(f"SELECT matricule,student_id,school_email,profile_pic,enrollment_year FROM students WHERE student_id ={id}")
        student = self.cursor.fetchone()
        return student
    def fetch_hod_by_teacher_id(self,id:int):
        self.cursor.execute(f"SELECT id,teacher_id,department_id FROM hods WHERE teacher_id ={id}")
        hod = self.cursor.fetchone()
        return hod
    def fetch_staff_by_teacher_id(self,id:int):
        self.cursor.execute(f"""SELECT staff.id,staff.teacher_id,teachers.department_id 
                            FROM staff INNER JOIN teachers ON staff.teacher_id = teachers.teacher_id  
                            AND staff.department_id = teachers.department_id  WHERE staff.teacher_id ={id}""")
        staff = self.cursor.fetchone()
        return staff
    
    
    
    # STRUCTURE FETCHES
    def fetch_school(self):
        self.cursor.execute("SELECT id,name,logo,address from schools")
        schools = self.cursor.fetchall()
        return schools
    def fetch_department(self):
        self.cursor.execute("SELECT id,name,logo,code,school_id from departments")
        departments = self.cursor.fetchall()
        return departments
    def fetch_level_degree(self):
        self.cursor.execute("SELECT id,name,code from level_degrees")
        level_degree = self.cursor.fetchall()
        return level_degree
    def fetch_level_department(self):
        self.cursor.execute("SELECT id,department_id,level_degree_id,num_levels FROM department_degrees")
        level_department = self.cursor.fetchall()
        return level_department
    def fetch_class(self):
        self.cursor.execute("SELECT id,name,level_number,department_degree_id FROM classes")
        classes = self.cursor.fetchall()
        return classes
    
    
    # Fetch the Academics models
    def fetch_course(self):
        self.cursor.execute("SELECT id,code,name,description FROM courses")
        courses = self.cursor.fetchall()
        
        return courses
    
    def fetch_course_type(self):
        self.cursor.execute("SELECT id,name,description FROM course_categories")
        
        c_types = self.cursor.fetchall()
        
        return c_types
    
    def fetch_class_course(self):
        self.cursor.execute("SELECT id,class_id,course_id,category_id,academic_year,semester_num,credit FROM class_courses")
        class_courses = self.cursor.fetchall()
        
        return class_courses
    
    def fetch_exam(self):
        self.cursor.execute("SELECT id,class_course_id FROM exams")
        
        exams = self.cursor.fetchall()
        
        return exams
    
    def fetch_exam_type(self):
        self.cursor.execute("SELECT id, name, description FROM exam_types")
        
        exam_types = self.cursor.fetchall()
        
        return exam_types
    
    def fetch_exam_exam_type(self):
        self.cursor.execute("SELECT id,exam_id,exam_type_id,exam_date,weight_percentage,max_marks FROM exam_components")
        exam_exam_types = self.cursor.fetchall()
        
        return exam_exam_types
    
    # Fetch teachers assigned to courses in a class
    def fetch_teacher_assignment(self):
        self.cursor.execute("SELECT id,teacher_id,class_course_id,academic_year,semester_num FROM teacher_assignments")
        return self.cursor.fetchall()
    # STUDENT ENROLLED IN CLASSES
    def fetch_student_class_course(self):
        self.cursor.execute("SELECT id,student_id,class_course_id,status,enrollment_date FROM course_enrollments")
        student_class_courses = self.cursor.fetchall()
        
        return student_class_courses

File: pairing.py | Lines: 23
Path: backend\synchronisation\pairing.py
------------------------------------------------------------
from .fetch import FetchData

fetchdata = FetchData()
class Pairer:
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.user_role_pair = []
        
    def user_type_pairer(self):
        users = fetchdata.fetch_user()
        
        for user in users:
            id,fullname,email,phone,gender,dob,user_type = user
            if user_type == "Admin":
                director = fetchdata.fetch_director_by_id(id)
                self.user_role_pair.append((user,director))
            if user_type == "Teacher":
                teacher = fetchdata.fetch_teacher_by_id(id)
                self.user_role_pair.append((user,teacher))
            if user_type == "Student":
                student = fetchdata.fetch_student_by_id(id)
                self.user_role_pair.append((user,student))
        return self.user_role_pair

from user.models import *
from structure.models import *
from academics.models import *
from .pairing import Pairer
from .fetch import FetchData
from .checks import TeacherRole
# TO manage images
from django.core.files.base import ContentFile
from PIL import Image
import io
import base64
# instantiate the Pairer class & FetchData class
pairer = Pairer()
fetchdata = FetchData()
teacher_role = TeacherRole()
# Synchronisation class
class Sync:
    def sync_user(self):
        users_pair_role = pairer.user_type_pairer()
        
        for pair in users_pair_role:
            user,role = pair
            id,fullname,email,phone,gender,dob,user_type = user
            
            # unpacking first and last name
            parts = fullname.split()
            
            # Extract the password from the role pair
            password = role[0]
            
            db_user,created = User.objects.update_or_create(
                external_id = id,
                defaults={
                    'username': parts[0]+"."+"".join(parts[1:]),
                    'first_name': parts[0],
                    'last_name': " ".join(parts[1:]),
                    'email': email,
                    'phone': phone,
                    'gender': gender,
                    'dob': dob,
                    'user_type': user_type
                }
            )
            
            if created:
                db_user.set_password(password)
                
            db_user.save()
        print("===========================")
        print("User Synchronisation Done!!")
        print("===========================")
        return True
# Teacher's Role synchronisations
    def sync_user_role(self):
        pairs = pairer.user_type_pairer()
        for pair in pairs:
            user,role = pair
            id,fullname,email,phone,gender,dob,user_type = user
            if user_type == 'Admin':
                matricule,admin_id,school_id,profile_pic = role
                
                user_model = User.objects.filter(external_id=id).first()
                school_model = School.objects.filter(external_id=school_id).first()
                db_director,created = Director.objects.update_or_create(
                    external_id = admin_id,
                    defaults={
                        "matricule": matricule,
                        "user": user_model,
                        "school": school_model,
                    }
                )
            
                if profile_pic:
                    
                    img = Image.open(io.BytesIO(profile_pic))
                    ext = img.format.lower()
                    filename = f"admin_{admin_id}_profile_pic.{ext}"
                    db_director.profile_pic.save(filename,ContentFile(profile_pic),save=True)
                    print("===========================")
                    print("Admins Synchronisation Done!!")
                    print("===========================")
                db_director.save()
                
                    
            elif user_type == 'Teacher':
                # print(user)
                matricule,teacher_id,school_email,department_id,profile_pic = role
                user_model = User.objects.filter(external_id=id).first()
                dept_model = Department.objects.filter(external_id=department_id).first()
                db_teacher,created = Teacher.objects.update_or_create(
                    external_id = teacher_id,
                    defaults={
                        "matricule": matricule,
                        "school_mail": school_email,
                        "user": user_model, 
                        "department": dept_model,
                        "is_hod": teacher_role.isHod(teacher_id),
                        "is_staff": teacher_role.isStaff(teacher_id)
                    }
                )
                # Save first the teacher before saving the specific role
                
                if profile_pic:
                    img = Image.open(io.BytesIO(profile_pic))
                    ext = img.format.lower()
                    filename = f"teacher_{teacher_id}_profile_pic.{ext}"
                    db_teacher.profile_pic.save(filename,ContentFile(profile_pic),save=True)
                    
                    print("===========================")
                    print("Teachers Synchronisation Done!!")
                    print("===========================")
                db_teacher.save()
                    
                if teacher_role.isHod(teacher_id):
                    t = fetchdata.fetch_hod_by_teacher_id(teacher_id)
                    t_id,t_id,t_department_id = t
                    t_dpt = Department.objects.filter(external_id=t_department_id).first()
                    t_tch_id = Teacher.objects.filter(external_id=t_id).first()
                    db_hod,hod_created = HOD.objects.update_or_create(
                        external_id = t_id,
                        defaults={
                            'department': t_dpt,
                            'teacher': t_tch_id
                        }
                    )
                    
                    db_hod.save()
                        
                elif teacher_role.isStaff(teacher_id):
                    t = fetchdata.fetch_staff_by_teacher_id(teacher_id)
                    t_id,t_id,t_department_id = t
                    t_dpt = Department.objects.filter(external_id=t_department_id).first()
                    t_tch_id = Teacher.objects.filter(external_id=t_id).first()
                    db_staff,staff_created = Staff.objects.update_or_create(
                        external_id = t_id,
                        defaults={
                            'department': t_dpt,
                            'teacher': t_tch_id
                        }
                    )
                    if staff_created:
                        db_staff.save()
                        
                else:
                    pass
                
            elif user_type == 'Student':
                # print(user)
                matricule,student_id,school_email,profile_pic,enrollment_year = role
                user_model = User.objects.filter(external_id=id).first()
                db_student,created = Student.objects.update_or_create(
                    external_id = student_id,
                    defaults={
                        "matricule": matricule,
                        "school_mail": school_email,
                        "user": user_model,
                        "enrolled_year": enrollment_year
                    }
                )
               
                if profile_pic:
                    img = Image.open(io.BytesIO(profile_pic))
                    ext = img.format.lower()
                    filename = f"student_{student_id}_profile_pic.{ext}"
                    db_student.profile_pic.save(filename,ContentFile(profile_pic),save=True)
                    
                    print("===========================")
                    print("Students Synchronisation Done!!")
                    print("===========================")
                db_student.save()
                    
                    
                    
            else:
                pass
        print("===========================")
        print("Roles Synchronisation Done!!")
        print("===========================")
        
    def sync_structure(self,model:str):
        
        if model == "School":
            schools = fetchdata.fetch_school()
            
            for school in schools:
                id,name,logo,code = school
                
                db_school,created = School.objects.update_or_create(
                    external_id = id,
                    defaults={
                        'name': name,
                        'code': code
                    }
                )
                
                if logo:
                    img = Image.open(io.BytesIO(logo))
                    ext = img.format.lower()
                    filename = f"school_{id}_logo.{ext}"
                    db_school.logo.save(filename,ContentFile(logo),save=True)
                
                db_school.save()
            print("===========================")
            print("Schools sync Done!")
            print("===========================")
            
        if model == "Department":
            departments = fetchdata.fetch_department()
            
            for department in departments:
                id,name,logo,code,school_id = department
                sch = School.objects.filter(external_id=school_id).first()
                db_department,created = Department.objects.update_or_create(
                    external_id = id,
                    defaults={
                        'name': name,
                        'code': code,
                        'school': sch
                    }
                )
                
                if logo:
                    img = Image.open(io.BytesIO(logo))
                    ext = img.format.lower()
                    filename = f"department_{id}_logo.{ext}"
                    db_department.logo.save(filename,ContentFile(logo),save=True)
                db_department.save()
            print("===========================")
            print("Departments sync Done!")
            print("===========================")
        if model == "LevelDregee":
            level_degrees = fetchdata.fetch_level_degree()
            
            for level_degree in level_degrees:
                id,name,code = level_degree
                db_level_degree,created = LevelDegree.objects.update_or_create(
                    external_id = id,
                    defaults={
                        'name': name,
                        'code': code,
                    }
                )
                
                db_level_degree.save()
            print("===========================")
            print("LevelDegree sync Done!")
            print("===========================")
        if model == "DepartmentLevelDegree":
            department_degrees = fetchdata.fetch_level_department()
            
            for department_degree in department_degrees:
                id,dept_id,level_id,num_levels = department_degree
                dept = Department.objects.filter(external_id=dept_id).first()
                level_deg = LevelDegree.objects.filter(external_id=level_id).first()
                db_dept_degree,created = DepartmentLevelDegree.objects.update_or_create(
                    external_id = id,
                    defaults={
                        'department': dept,
                        'level_degree': level_deg,
                        'num_levels':num_levels
                    }
                )
                
                if created:
                    db_dept_degree.save()
            print("===========================")
            print("DepartmentDegree sync Done!")
            print("===========================")
        if model == "Class":
            classes = fetchdata.fetch_class()
                        
            for _class in classes:
                id,name,level_number,department_degree_id = _class
                dept_deg = DepartmentLevelDegree.objects.filter(external_id=department_degree_id).first()
                db_class,created = Class.objects.update_or_create(
                    external_id = id,
                    defaults={
                        'name': name,
                        'level': level_number,
                        'department_level_degree': dept_deg
                    }
                )
                
                
                db_class.save()
            print("===========================")
            print("Classes sync Done!")
            print("===========================")
            
    def sync_academic(self,model:str):
        
        if model == "Course":
            courses = fetchdata.fetch_course()
            
            for course in courses:
                id,code,name,description = course
                
                db_course,created = Course.objects.update_or_create(
                    external_id = id,
                    defaults={
                        "code":code,
                        "name":name,
                        "desc":description
                    }
                )
                
                if created:
                    db_course.save()
                
            print("===========================")
            print("Courses sync Done!")
            print("===========================")
            
        if model == "CourseType":
            course_types = fetchdata.fetch_course_type()
            
            for course_type in course_types:
                id,name,description = course_type
                
                db_course_type,created = CourseType.objects.update_or_create(
                    external_id = id,
                    defaults={
                        "name":name,
                        "desc":description
                    }
                )
                
            
                db_course_type.save()
                
            print("===========================")
            print("CourseTypes sync Done!")
            print("===========================")
            
        if model == "ClassCourse":
            class_courses = fetchdata.fetch_class_course()
            
            for class_course in class_courses:
                id,class_id,course_id,category_id,academy_year,semester_num,credit = class_course
                
                _class = Class.objects.filter(external_id=class_id).first()
                course = Course.objects.filter(external_id=course_id).first()
                course_type = CourseType.objects.filter(external_id=category_id).first()
                db_class_course,created = ClassCourse.objects.update_or_create(
                    external_id = id,
                    defaults={
                        "_class":_class,
                        "course":course,
                        "course_type":course_type,
                        "credit":credit,
                        "academic_year": academy_year,
                        "semester_num": semester_num
                    }
                )
                
                
                db_class_course.save()
                
            print("===========================")
            print("CourseClasses sync Done!")
            print("===========================")
        
        
        if model == "Exam":
            exams = fetchdata.fetch_exam()
            
            for exam in exams:
                id,class_course_id = exam
                
                class_course = ClassCourse.objects.filter(external_id=class_course_id).first()
                db_exam,created = Exam.objects.update_or_create(
                    external_id = id,
                    defaults={
                        "class_course":class_course
                    }
                )
                
                
                db_exam.save()
                
            print("===========================")
            print("Exams sync Done!")
            print("===========================")
        if model == "ExamType":
            exam_types = fetchdata.fetch_exam_type()
            
            for exam_type in exam_types:
                id, name, description = exam_type
                
                db_exam_type,created = ExamType.objects.update_or_create(
                    external_id = id,
                    defaults={
                        "name":name
                    }
                )
                
                db_exam_type.save()
                
            print("===========================")
            print("ExamTypes sync Done!")
            print("===========================")
        if model == "ExamExamType":
            exam_exam_types = fetchdata.fetch_exam_exam_type()
            
            for exam_exam_type in exam_exam_types:
                id,exam_id,exam_type_id,exam_date,weight_percentage,max_mark = exam_exam_type
                exam = Exam.objects.filter(external_id=exam_id).first()
                exam_type = ExamType.objects.filter(external_id=exam_type_id).first()
                db_exam_exam_type,created = ExamExamType.objects.update_or_create(
                    external_id = id,
                    defaults={
                        "exam":exam,
                        "exam_type":exam_type,
                        "exam_date":exam_date,
                        "weight_percent": weight_percentage,
                        "max_mark": max_mark
                    }
                )
                
                db_exam_exam_type.save()
                
            print("===========================")
            print("ExamExamTypes sync Done!")
            print("===========================")
        if model == "TeacherAssignment":
            teacher_assignments = fetchdata.fetch_teacher_assignment()
            
            for teacher_assingment in teacher_assignments:
                id,teacher_id,class_course_id,academic_year,semester_num = teacher_assingment
                teacher = Teacher.objects.filter(external_id=teacher_id).first()
                class_course = ClassCourse.objects.filter(external_id=class_course_id).first()
                
                db_teacher_assignment,created = TeacherAssignment.objects.update_or_create(
                    external_id = id,
                    defaults = {
                        "teacher":teacher,
                        "class_course":class_course,
                        "academic_year":academic_year,
                        "semester_num":semester_num
                    }
                )
                
                db_teacher_assignment.save()
                
            print("===========================")
            print("Teacher Assigned sync Done!")
            print("===========================")
                
        if model == "StudentClassCourse":
            student_class_courses = fetchdata.fetch_student_class_course()
            
            for student_class_course in student_class_courses:
                id,student_id,class_course_id,status,enrollment_date = student_class_course
                
                student = Student.objects.filter(external_id=student_id).first()
                class_course = ClassCourse.objects.filter(external_id=class_course_id).first()
                db_student_class_course,created = StudentClassCourse.objects.update_or_create(
                    external_id = id,
                    defaults={
                        "student":student,
                        "class_course":class_course,
                        "status":status,
                        "enrolled_date": enrollment_date
                    }
                )
                
                db_student_class_course.save()
                
            print("===========================")
            print("Student Enrollment sync Done!")
            print("===========================")

from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.decorators import api_view, permission_classes
from rest_framework.permissions import AllowAny
from .sync import Sync

sync = Sync()
@api_view(["GET"])
# @permission_classes([AllowAny])
def sync_data(request):
    structure_models = ["School","Department","LevelDregee","DepartmentLevelDegree","Class"]
    academics_models = ["Course","CourseType","ClassCourse","Exam","ExamType","ExamExamType","StudentClassCourse","TeacherAssignment"]
    sync.sync_user()
    for model in structure_models:
        sync.sync_structure(model)
    sync.sync_user_role()
    for model in academics_models:
        sync.sync_academic(model)
    return Response({"message":"Data Successfully fetched!!"})

from django.db import models
# Import the AbstractUser from django model
from django.contrib.auth.models import AbstractUser
from structure.models import Department,School

# Import the images overwriter
from core.image_write import OverWriteStorage

# The Signature model
class Signature(models.Model):
    image = models.ImageField(upload_to="signatures/",null=False,blank=False)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(null=False,blank=False,auto_now_add=True)
    upload_at = models.DateTimeField(null=False,blank=False,auto_now=True)
    

# The Main User class model
class User(AbstractUser):
    # The 3 main roles derived from user model
    class UserType(models.TextChoices):
        DIRECTOR = "Admin"
        TEACHER = "Teacher"
        STUDENT = "Student"
        
    # Status of each user in the system
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        ACTIVE = "Active"
        SUSPENDED = "Suspended"
        
    
    # Gender choices for the user model
    class Gender(models.TextChoices):
        MALE = "Male"
        FEMALE = "Female"
        OTHER = "Other"
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=True,blank=True)
    user_type = models.CharField(choices=UserType.choices,null=False)
    phone = models.CharField(max_length=20,null=False,blank=False)
    dob = models.DateField(null=False,blank=False)
    gender = models.CharField(max_length=10,choices=Gender.choices,null=False,blank=False)
    status = models.CharField(choices=Status.choices,null=False,blank=False,default=Status.ACTIVE)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(blank=True,null=True,auto_now_add=True)
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.username} - {self.user_type}"
    def fullname(self):
        return f"{self.first_name} {self.last_name}".strip().upper()


# The Director Model director of schools
class Director(models.Model):
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=True,blank=True)
    matricule = models.CharField(max_length=20,null=False,blank=False)
    user = models.OneToOneField(User,on_delete=models.CASCADE,related_name="director")
    school = models.OneToOneField(School,on_delete=models.SET_NULL,blank=True,null=True,related_name='director')
    signature = models.OneToOneField(Signature,on_delete=models.SET_NULL,null=True,blank=True)
    profile_pic = models.ImageField(upload_to="directors/profile_pics/",null=True,blank=True,storage=OverWriteStorage())
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.username} - Director"

# The Teacher model that's the second user extension
class Teacher(models.Model):
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=True,blank=True)
    matricule = models.CharField(max_length=20,blank=False,null=False)
    school_mail = models.CharField(max_length=45,null=True,blank=True)
    is_hod = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    is_staff = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    user = models.OneToOneField(User,on_delete=models.CASCADE,related_name="teacher")
    department = models.ForeignKey(Department,on_delete=models.SET_NULL,null=True,blank=True)
    profile_pic = models.ImageField(upload_to="teachers/profile_pics/",null=True,blank=True,storage=OverWriteStorage())
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.username} - Teacher"

# The Teacher's Derived Models
# - HOD Model:
class HOD(models.Model):
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=True,blank=True)
    teacher = models.OneToOneField(Teacher,on_delete=models.CASCADE,related_name="hod")
    signature = models.OneToOneField(Signature,on_delete=models.SET_NULL,null=True,blank=True)
    department = models.OneToOneField(Department,on_delete=models.CASCADE,blank=True,null=True,related_name="hod")
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.teacher.user.username} - HOD"

# - Staff model
class Staff(models.Model):
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=True,blank=True)
    teacher = models.OneToOneField(Teacher,on_delete=models.CASCADE,related_name="staff")
    signature = models.OneToOneField(Signature,on_delete=models.SET_NULL,null=True,blank=True)
    department = models.ForeignKey(Department,on_delete=models.CASCADE,blank=True,null=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.teacher.user.username} - Staff"
    

# The Student Model
class Student(models.Model):
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=True,blank=True)
    matricule = models.CharField(max_length=20,null=False,blank=False)
    school_mail = models.CharField(max_length=50,null=False,blank=False)
    enrolled_year = models.IntegerField(null=False,blank=False)
    user = models.OneToOneField(User,on_delete=models.CASCADE,related_name="student")
    profile_pic = models.ImageField(upload_to="students/profile_pics/",null=True,blank=True,storage=OverWriteStorage())
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.username} - Student"
    
    
# Student in class and course (Enrollements)
class StudentClassCourse(models.Model):
    
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        ENROLLED = "Enrolled"
        COMPLETED = "Completed"
        SUSPENDED = "Suspended"
    external_id = models.IntegerField(null=True,blank=True)
    student = models.ForeignKey(Student,on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    class_course = models.ForeignKey("academics.ClassCourse",on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    status = models.CharField(choices=Status.choices,blank=False,null=False,default=Status.ENROLLED)
    enrolled_date = models.DateField()
    
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"The student {self.student.matricule} is in {self.class_course._class.name} and offer {self.class_course.course.name}"

from rest_framework import serializers
from django.contrib.auth import authenticate
from rest_framework_simplejwt.serializers import TokenObtainPairSerializer
from structure.serializers import SchoolSerializer
# Impmort the models to serialize
from .models import *

from synchronisation.sync import Sync

# credential confirmation method
def confirm_credential(login_input):
    if "@" in login_input: 
        user = User.objects.filter(email=login_input).first()
        return user
    else:
        user = User.objects.filter(phone=login_input).first()
        return user
    return user
# User ModelSerializer 
class UserSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    
    class Meta:
        model = User
        fields = ["id",
                  "username",
                  "first_name",
                  "last_name",
                  "password",
                  "email",
                  "phone",
                  "dob",
                  "user_type",
                  "status",
                  "last_login",
                  "created_at"]
        extra_kwargs = {"password":{"write_only":True}}
        

class PasswordUpdateSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    
    email = serializers.EmailField()
    password = serializers.CharField(write_only=True)
    new_password = serializers.CharField(write_only=True)
    
    class Meta:
        model = User
        fields = ["email","password","new_password"]
        
    def update(self, instance, validated_data):
        email = validated_data.get("email")
        password = validated_data.get("password")
        new_password = validated_data.get("new_password")
        
        # check email
        if instance.email != email:
            raise serializers.ValidationError("Wrong Crdentials")
        
        if not instance.check_password(password):
            raise serializers.ValidationError("Wrong Credentials")
        
        instance.set_password(new_password)
        instance.save()
        
        return instance

class PasswordResetSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = User
        fields = ["email"]
    
    def update(self,instance,validated_data):
        email = validated_data.get("email")
        
        if instance.user_type == "Admin":
            role = Director.objects.filter(user=instance).first()
        elif instance.user_type == "Teacher":
            role = Teacher.objects.filter(user=instance).first()
        else:
            role = Student.objects.filter(user=instance).first()
        
        instance.set_password(role.matricule)
        instance.save()
        return instance
    
class CustomTokenObtainPairSerializer(TokenObtainPairSerializer):
    

    def validate(self,attrs):
        
        login_input = attrs.get("username")
        password = attrs.get("password")
        
        if not confirm_credential(login_input):
            sync = Sync()
            is_sync = sync.sync_user()
            
        user = confirm_credential(login_input)
        
        if user and user.check_password(password):
            refresh = self.get_token(user)
            return {
                "refresh": str(refresh),
                "access": str(refresh.access_token),
                "user": UserSerializer(user).data
            }
        
        raise serializers.ValidationError("Invalid credentials")

class DirectorSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    profile_pic = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    school = SchoolSerializer()
    class Meta:
        model = Director
        fields ="__all__"
    
    def get_profile_pic(self, obj):
        request = self.context.get("request")
        if obj.profile_pic and hasattr(obj.profile_pic, "url"):
            return request.build_absolute_uri(obj.profile_pic.url)
        return None

from urllib import request

from django.shortcuts import render
from core.permissions import IsStudent
from rest_framework_simplejwt.views import TokenObtainPairView

from .serializers import *
from .models import User, Signature
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.generics import RetrieveUpdateAPIView,RetrieveAPIView

from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.parsers import MultiPartParser, FormParser
from academics.models import ClassCourse
from structure import models,serializers

class CustomTokenObtainPairView(TokenObtainPairView):
    serializer_class = CustomTokenObtainPairSerializer

    serializer_class = PasswordUpdateSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

        return self.request.user

# The student enrollement view
# Getting the student's courses based on academic_year
# Also getting the students in a class
class StudentClassCourseView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    
    def get(self,request,academic_year=None,class_id=None):
        
        user = self.request.user
        if class_id:
            _class = models.Class.objects.filter(id=class_id).first()
            student_class_courses = StudentClassCourse.objects.filter(class_course___class=_class)
            data = []
            for s_c_c in student_class_courses:
                student = Student.objects.filter(id=s_c_c.student.id).first()
                if not any(d["student_name"] == student.user.fullname() 
                for d in data):
                    data.append({
                        "id": s_c_c.id,
                        "student_id": student.id,
                        "student_name": student.user.fullname(),
                        "student_matricule": student.matricule,
                        "student_email": student.school_mail,
                        "enrolled_year": student.enrolled_year
                    }) 
            return Response(data)
        
        if academic_year and hasattr(user,"student"):
            formated_year = f"{academic_year-1}/{academic_year}"
            student = Student.objects.filter(user=user).first()
            
            student_class_courses = StudentClassCourse.objects.filter(student=student,class_course__academic_year=formated_year)
            data =[]
            for s_c_c in student_class_courses:
                class_course = ClassCourse.objects.filter(id=s_c_c.class_course.id).first()
                _class = models.Class.objects.filter(id=class_course._class.id).first()
                data.append({
                    "id":s_c_c.id,
                    "course_name": s_c_c.class_course.course.name,
                    "course_type": s_c_c.class_course.course_type.name,
                    "credit": s_c_c.class_course.credit,
                    "academic_year": formated_year,
                    "semester_num": s_c_c.class_course.semester_num,
                    "class": _class.name
                    
                })
            
            return Response(data)
            

class UserInformationView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    def get_serializer_context(self):
        return {"request": self.request}
    
    def get(self,request):
        user = self.request.user
        data = {}
        if hasattr(user,'student'):
            data["matricule"] = user.student.matricule
            data["role"] = "Student"
            data["profile"] = request.build_absolute_uri(user.student.profile_pic.url) if user.student.profile_pic else None
        elif hasattr(user,'director'):
            data["matricule"] = user.director.matricule
            data["role"] = "Director"
            data["school"] = user.director.school.name
            data["profile"] = request.build_absolute_uri(user.director.profile_pic.url) if user.director.profile_pic else None
        else:
            data["matricule"] = user.teacher.matricule
            data["role"] = "HOD" if user.teacher.is_hod else "Staff"
            data["profile"] = request.build_absolute_uri(user.teacher.profile_pic.url) if user.teacher.profile_pic else None
            data["department_id"] = user.teacher.department.id if user.teacher.department else None
        
        data.update({
            "id": user.id,
            "fullname": user.fullname(),
            "gender": user.gender,
            "email": user.email,
            "phone": user.phone,
            "dob": user.dob,
            "is_active": user.is_active
            
        })
        
        return Response(data)

class SignatureUploadView(APIView):
    """Upload or replace signature for Director, HOD, or Staff."""
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    parser_classes = [MultiPartParser, FormParser]

    def get(self, request):
        user = request.user
        sig = None
        if hasattr(user, 'director') and user.director.signature:
            sig = user.director.signature
        elif hasattr(user, 'teacher'):
            if user.teacher.is_hod and hasattr(user.teacher, 'hod') and user.teacher.hod.signature:
                sig = user.teacher.hod.signature
            elif user.teacher.is_staff and hasattr(user.teacher, 'staff') and user.teacher.staff.signature:
                sig = user.teacher.staff.signature
        if sig:
            return Response({"signature": request.build_absolute_uri(sig.image.url)})
        return Response({"signature": None})

    def post(self, request):
        user = request.user
        image = request.FILES.get("signature")
        if not image:
            return Response({"error": "No signature image provided."}, status=400)

        sig = Signature.objects.create(image=image)

        if hasattr(user, 'director'):
            user.director.signature = sig
            user.director.save()
        elif hasattr(user, 'teacher'):
            if user.teacher.is_hod and hasattr(user.teacher, 'hod'):
                user.teacher.hod.signature = sig
                user.teacher.hod.save()
            elif user.teacher.is_staff and hasattr(user.teacher, 'staff'):
                user.teacher.staff.signature = sig
                user.teacher.staff.save()
            else:
                return Response({"error": "Only HOD, Staff, and Director can upload signatures."}, status=403)
        else:
            return Response({"error": "Students cannot upload signatures."}, status=403)

        return Response({"message": "Signature uploaded successfully.", "signature": request.build_absolute_uri(sig.image.url)})

# Results Remark 
from decimal import Decimal

from academics.models import ExamResult


def compute_remark(max_mark:Decimal,mark: Decimal) -> str:
    compute_mark = (mark/max_mark) * 100
    if compute_mark >= 90:
        return ExamResult.Remark.EXCELLENT
    elif compute_mark >= 80:
        return ExamResult.Remark.VERYGOOD
    elif compute_mark >= 70:
        return ExamResult.Remark.GOOD
    elif compute_mark >= 60:
        return ExamResult.Remark.FAIRLYGOOD
    elif compute_mark >= 50:
        return ExamResult.Remark.AVERAGE
    else:
        return ExamResult.Remark.WEAK

# React + Vite

This template provides a minimal setup to get React working in Vite with HMR and some ESLint rules.

Currently, two official plugins are available:

- [@vitejs/plugin-react](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react) uses [Babel](https://babeljs.io/) for Fast Refresh
- [@vitejs/plugin-react-swc](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react-swc) uses [SWC](https://swc.rs/) for Fast Refresh

## Expanding the ESLint configuration

If you are developing a production application, we recommend using TypeScript with type-aware lint rules enabled. Check out the [TS template](https://github.com/vitejs/vite/tree/main/packages/create-vite/template-react-ts) for information on how to integrate TypeScript and [`typescript-eslint`](https://typescript-eslint.io) in your project.

/** @format */

import styled from "styled-components";
import DirectorDashboard from "./page/director/DirectorDashboard";
import StudentDashboard from "./page/student/StudentDashboard";
import StudentResult from "./page/student/StudentResult";
import StudentComplaint from "./page/student/StudentComplaint";
import StudentCourses from "./page/student/StudentCourses";
import StudentTranscript from "./page/student/StudentTranscript";
import StaffTranscripts from "./page/hod_staff/StaffTranscripts";
import Login from "./page/auth/Login";
import { BrowserRouter as Router, Route, Routes } from "react-router-dom";
import PrivateRoutes from "./components/PrivateRoutes";
import HodStaffDashboard from "./page/hod_staff/HodStaffDashboard";
import HodStaffResults from "./page/hod_staff/HodStaffResults";
import Layout from "./components/Layout";
import "./index.css";
import Department from "./page/director/Department";
import LevelDegree from "./page/director/LevelDegree";
import Profile from "./page/user/Profile";
import PasswordReset from "./page/user/PasswordReset";
import Class from "./page/hod_staff/Class";
import StaffCourses from "./page/hod_staff/StaffCourses";
import ClassDetail from "./page/hod_staff/ClassDetail";
import DepartmentVisualization from "./page/hod_staff/DepartmentVisualization";
import StaffComplaints from "./page/hod_staff/StaffComplaints";
import Announcements from "./page/user/Announcements";
import CreateAnnouncement from "./page/user/CreateAnnouncement";
import Timetables from "./page/user/Timetables";
import CreateTimetable from "./page/user/CreateTimetable";
import NotFound from "./page/user/NotFound";

const Container = styled.div`
  
`;

function AppContent() {
  return (
    <Container>
      <Routes>
        <Route
          path="/login"
          element={<Login />} />
        <Route
          path="*"
          element={
            <Layout>
              <NotFound />
            </Layout>
          } />
        {/* Common user route */}
        <Route
          path="/profile"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Admin","Teacher","Student"]}>
              <Layout>
                <Profile />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        <Route
          path="/update/password"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Admin","Teacher","Student"]}>
              <Layout>
                <PasswordReset />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        {/* Common user routes */}
        <Route
          path="/announcements"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Admin","Teacher","Student"]}>
              <Layout>
                <Announcements />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        <Route
          path="/create-announcement"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Admin","Teacher"]}>
              <Layout>
                <CreateAnnouncement />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        <Route
          path="/timetables"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Admin","Teacher","Student"]}>
              <Layout>
                <Timetables />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        <Route
          path="/create-timetable"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Admin","Teacher"]}>
              <Layout>
                <CreateTimetable />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        <Route
          path="/not-found"
          element={
              <Layout>
                <NotFound/>
              </Layout>
          } />
          
        {/* Admin's routes */}
        <Route
          path="/director/dashboard"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Admin"]}>
              <Layout>
                <DirectorDashboard />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        <Route
          path="/director/level-degree"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Admin"]}>
              <Layout>
                <LevelDegree />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        <Route
          path="/director/department"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Admin"]}>
              <Layout>
                <Department />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        {/* Student's routes */}
        <Route
          path="/student/dashboard"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Student"]}>
              <Layout>
                <StudentDashboard />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        <Route
          path="/student/result"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Student"]}>
              <Layout>
                <StudentResult />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        <Route
          path="/student/complaint"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Student"]}>
              <Layout>
                <StudentComplaint />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        {/* hod's or staff's routes */}
        <Route
          path="/hod/staff/dashboard"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Teacher"]}>
              <Layout>
                <HodStaffDashboard />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        <Route
          path="/hod/staff/results"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Teacher"]}>
              <Layout>
                <HodStaffResults />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        <Route
          path="/hod/staff/class"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Teacher"]}>
              <Layout>
                <Class />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        <Route
          path="/hod/staff/class/:id"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Teacher"]}>
              <Layout>
                <ClassDetail />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        <Route
          path="/hod/staff/courses"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Teacher"]}>
              <Layout>
                <StaffCourses />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        <Route
          path="/hod/staff/department-visualization"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Teacher"]}>
              <Layout>
                <DepartmentVisualization />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        <Route
          path="/hod/staff/complaints"
          element={
            <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Teacher"]}>
              <Layout>
                <StaffComplaints />
              </Layout>
            </PrivateRoutes>
          } />
        <Route path="/student/courses" element={
          <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Student"]}>
            <Layout>
              <StudentCourses />
            </Layout>
          </PrivateRoutes>} />
        <Route path="/student/transcript" element={
          <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Student"]}>
            <Layout>
              <StudentTranscript />
            </Layout>
          </PrivateRoutes>} />
        <Route path="/hod/staff/transcripts" element={
          <PrivateRoutes allowedRoles={["Admin","Teacher"]}>
            <Layout>
              <StaffTranscripts />
            </Layout>
          </PrivateRoutes>} />
      </Routes>
    </Container>
  )
}
function App() {
  return (
    <Router>
      <AppContent />
    </Router>
  );
}

export default App;

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import styled from 'styled-components';
import { logOut } from '../services/auth';
import { MdClass, MdDashboard, MdReportProblem, MdSchool } from "react-icons/md"
import { FaBook, FaBuilding, FaCalendar, FaChartBar, FaExclamationTriangle, FaGraduationCap, FaSchool, FaTachometerAlt, FaUser } from "react-icons/fa"
import api from '../services/api';
import { SyncOverlay } from './UI';
import { useSync } from '../context/SyncContext';
import { FiLogOut } from 'react-icons/fi';

const LayoutContainer = styled.div`
  display: flex;
  min-height: 100vh;
  background-color: var(--bg-color);

  @media (max-width: 768px){
    flex-direction: column;
  }
`;

const Sidebar = styled.aside`
  width: 260px;

  background-color: var(--primary);
  color: white;
  display: flex;

const NavItem = styled.div`

  font-weight: 500;
  
  &:hover {
    background-color: rgba(255,255,255,0.1);

  }
  
`;

  @media (max-width: 768px){

const Layout = ({ children }) => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { isSyncing } = useSync();

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logOut();
  };

  const adminNavItems = [
    { id: "dashboard",    label: "Dashboard",    icon: <MdDashboard />,         route: "/director/dashboard" },
    { id: "departments",  label: "Departments",  icon: <FaBuilding />,          route: "/director/department" },
    { id: "level_degree", label: "Level Degrees",icon: <FaGraduationCap />,     route: "/director/level-degree" },
    { id: "transcripts",  label: "Transcripts",  icon: <FaGraduationCap />,     route: "/hod/staff/transcripts" },
    { id: "announcements",label: "Announcements",icon: <FaExclamationTriangle />,route: "/announcements" },
    { id: "timetables",   label: "Timetables",   icon: <FaCalendar />,          route: "/timetables" },
    { id: "profile",      label: "Profile",      icon: <FaUser />,              route: "/profile" },
  ];

  const hod_staffNavItems = [
    { id: "dashboard", label: "Dashboard", icon: <MdDashboard />, route: "/hod/staff/dashboard" },
    { id: "class", label: "Classes", icon: <MdClass />, route: "/hod/staff/class" },
    { id: "course", label: "Courses", icon: <FaBook />, route: "/hod/staff/courses" },
    { id: "result",      label: "Results",     icon: <FaChartBar />,          route: "/hod/staff/results" },
    { id: "transcripts", label: "Transcripts", icon: <FaGraduationCap />,      route: "/hod/staff/transcripts" },
    { id: "announcements",label:"Announcements",icon:<FaExclamationTriangle />, route:"/announcements" },
    { id: "timetables", label: "Timetables", icon: <FaCalendar />, route: "/timetables" },
    { id: "profile", label: "Profile", icon: <FaUser />, route: "/profile" },
  ]
  const studentNavItems = [
    { id: "dashboard",   label: "Dashboard",   icon: <MdDashboard />,       route: "/student/dashboard" },
    { id: "result",      label: "Results",     icon: <FaChartBar />,         route: "/student/result" },
    { id: "course",      label: "Courses",     icon: <FaBook />,             route: "/student/courses" },
    { id: "transcript",  label: "Transcript",  icon: <FaGraduationCap />,    route: "/student/transcript" },
    { id: "announcements",label:"Announcements",icon:<FaExclamationTriangle />,route:"/announcements" },
    { id: "timetables",  label: "Timetables",  icon: <FaCalendar />,         route: "/timetables" },
    { id: "complaint",   label: "Complaints",  icon: <MdReportProblem />,    route: "/student/complaint" },
    { id: "profile",     label: "Profile",     icon: <FaUser />,             route: "/profile" },
  ]
  const [user, setUser] = useState(null);
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState(null);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState("dashboard");

  useEffect(() => {
    const storedUser = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("user"));
    if (!storedUser) {
      navigate("/login");

    } else {
      setUser(storedUser);
      // Get the user's profile infos
      const fetchProfile = async () => {
        try {
          const res = await api.get("user/profile/");
          setProfile(res.data);
          console.log(profile);
        } catch (err) {
          console.log(`Profile error: ${err}`);
        }

      }
      fetchProfile();
      console.log(user);
    }
  }, [navigate]);
  return (
    <LayoutContainer >
      <Sidebar >
        <SidebarHeader>
          Uni<span>Portal</span>
        </SidebarHeader>
        <SidebarNav>
          {user?.user_type === "Admin" ? (
            adminNavItems.map((item) => (
              <NavItem key={item.id} className={`${activeTab === item.id ? "btn-accent" : ""}`} onClick={() => {
                navigate(item.route)
                setActiveTab(item.id)
              }}>
                {item.icon} <NavLabel>{item.label}</NavLabel>
              </NavItem>
            ))
          ) : ""}
          {user?.user_type === "Teacher" ? (
            hod_staffNavItems.map((item) => (
              <NavItem key={item.id} className={`${activeTab === item.id ? "btn-accent" : ""}`} onClick={() => {
                navigate(item.route)
                setActiveTab(item.id)
              }}>
                {item.icon} <NavLabel>{item.label}</NavLabel>
              </NavItem>
            ))
          ) : ""}
          {user?.user_type === "Student" ? (
            studentNavItems.map((item) => (
              <NavItem key={item.id} className={`${activeTab === item.id ? "btn-accent" : ""}`} onClick={() => {
                navigate(item.route)
                setActiveTab(item.id)
              }}>
                {item.icon} <NavLabel>{item.label}</NavLabel>
              </NavItem>
            ))
          ) : ""}
        </SidebarNav>
      </Sidebar>
      <MainContent>
        <TopHeader>
          <UserProfile>
            {profile ? (
              <>
                <div className='justify-content-start'>
                  <h3>{profile.role}</h3>
                </div>
                <div className='flex-col'>
                  <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontWeight: '500' }}>E-mail: {profile.email} </span>
                  <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontWeight: '500' }}>Matricule: {profile.matricule} </span>
                </div>
                <img src={profile.profile} alt="profile" style={{ width: "3rem", borderRadius: "50%" }} />
              </>
            ) : (
              <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontWeight: '500' }}>Profile</span>
            )}
            <LogoutButton onClick={handleLogout}><FiLogOut size={30} /></LogoutButton>
          </UserProfile>
        </TopHeader>
        <PageContent>
          {children}
        </PageContent>
      </MainContent>
      {isSyncing && <SyncOverlay />}
    </LayoutContainer>
  );
};

export default Layout;

File: PrivateRoutes.jsx | Lines: 18
Path: client\src\components\PrivateRoutes.jsx

import React from 'react'
import { Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';

function PrivateRoutes({allowedRoles,children}) {
const user = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("user"))
const token = localStorage.getItem("access")

if(!user || !token) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />

if (!allowedRoles.includes(user.user_type)){
    return <Navigate to="/not-found" replace/>
}

  return children;
}


export default PrivateRoutes

/**
 * UI.jsx — Shared components imported across the whole app.
 * Import what you need:
 *   import { Alert, Spinner, PageLoader, Card, Badge, StatusBadge,
 *            ActionBtn, HeroSection, SectionTitle, ModalOverlay } from '../components/UI';
 */

import React from 'react';
import styled, { keyframes, css } from 'styled-components';

// ─── Animations ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const fadeUp   = keyframes`from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(14px)}to{opacity:1;transform:none}`;
const fadeIn   = keyframes`from{opacity:0}to{opacity:1}`;
const spin     = keyframes`from{transform:rotate(0deg)}to{transform:rotate(360deg)}`;
const pulse    = keyframes`0%,100%{opacity:1}50%{opacity:.4}`;
const slideIn  = keyframes`from{transform:translateY(-12px);opacity:0}to{transform:none;opacity:1}`;

// ─── Alert / Message ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const ALERT_VARIANTS = {
  success: { bg: '#dcfce7', color: '#166534', border: '#bbf7d0', icon: '✓' },
  error:   { bg: '#fee2e2', color: '#dc2626', border: '#fecaca', icon: '✕' },
  warning: { bg: '#fef9c3', color: '#92400e', border: '#fde68a', icon: '⚠' },
  info:    { bg: '#e0f2fe', color: '#0369a1', border: '#bae6fd', icon: 'ℹ' },
};

const AlertWrap = styled.div`
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: .65rem;
  padding: .85rem 1.1rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border: 1px solid ${p => (ALERT_VARIANTS[p.$v] || ALERT_VARIANTS.info).border};
  background: ${p => (ALERT_VARIANTS[p.$v] || ALERT_VARIANTS.info).bg};
  color: ${p => (ALERT_VARIANTS[p.$v] || ALERT_VARIANTS.info).color};
  font-size: .875rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  animation: ${slideIn} .25s ease both;
  margin-bottom: ${p => p.$mb || '0'};
`;
const AlertIcon = styled.span`font-size:1rem;flex-shrink:0;margin-top:.05rem;`;
const AlertBody = styled.div`flex:1;line-height:1.5;`;
const AlertTitle= styled.div`font-weight:700;margin-bottom:.15rem;`;

/**
 * <Alert variant="success|error|warning|info" title="Optional title" mb="1rem">
 *   Message text here
 * </Alert>
 */
export const Alert = ({ variant = 'info', title, children, mb, style }) => {
  if (!children) return null;
  const v = ALERT_VARIANTS[variant] || ALERT_VARIANTS.info;
  return (
    <AlertWrap $v={variant} $mb={mb} style={style}>
      <AlertIcon>{v.icon}</AlertIcon>
      <AlertBody>
        {title && <AlertTitle>{title}</AlertTitle>}
        {children}
      </AlertBody>
    </AlertWrap>
  );
};

// ─── Spinner ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const SpinRing = styled.span`
  display: inline-block;
  width: ${p => p.$size || '20px'};
  height: ${p => p.$size || '20px'};
  border: ${p => p.$stroke || '2.5px'} solid ${p => p.$track || 'rgba(255,255,255,.3)'};
  border-top-color: ${p => p.$color || 'white'};
  border-radius: 50%;
  animation: ${spin} .7s linear infinite;
  flex-shrink: 0;
`;

/**
 * Inline spinner — adapts colour/size to context.
 * <Spinner />                        — small white (for buttons)
 * <Spinner color="#1e3a8a" track="#e2e8f0" size="28px" stroke="3px" />
 */
export const Spinner = ({ color, track, size, stroke, style }) => (
  <SpinRing $color={color} $track={track} $size={size} $stroke={stroke} style={style} />
);

// ─── Page-level loader (full page overlay while loading content) ──────────────
const LoaderWrap = styled.div`
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  min-height: ${p => p.$full ? '100vh' : '280px'};

`;
const LoaderRing = styled.div`

  height: ${p => p.$size || '40px'};
  border: 3.5px solid var(--border-color);
  border-top-color: var(--primary);

`;
const LoaderText = styled.p`

`;

 * <PageLoader size="56px" />
 */
export const PageLoader = ({ label = 'Loading…', full, size }) => (

File: SyncContext.jsx | Lines: 42
Path: client\src\context\SyncContext.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import api from '../services/api';

const SyncContext = createContext(null);

export const SyncProvider = ({ children }) => {
  const [isSyncing, setIsSyncing]     = useState(false);
  const [syncError, setSyncError]     = useState(null);
  const [syncSuccess, setSyncSuccess] = useState(false);
  // const [user, setUser] = useState(null);

  const startSync = useCallback(async () => {
    setIsSyncing(true);
    setSyncError(null);
    setSyncSuccess(false);
    // const storedUser = JSON.stringify(localStorage.getItem("user"));

    // setUser(storedUser);
    try {
      await api.get('sync/data');
      setSyncSuccess(true);
      setTimeout(() => setSyncSuccess(false), 3000);
    } catch (err) {
      setSyncError('Synchronisation failed. Please try again.');
      setTimeout(() => setSyncError(null), 4000);
    } finally {
      setIsSyncing(false);
    }
  }, []);

  return (
    <SyncContext.Provider value={{ isSyncing, syncError, syncSuccess, startSync }}>
      {children}
    </SyncContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useSync = () => {
  const ctx = useContext(SyncContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useSync must be used inside SyncProvider');
  return ctx;
};

------------------------------------------------------------
File: Login.jsx | Lines: 93
Path: client\src\page\auth\Login.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------
import axios from 'axios';
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { saveTokens } from '../../services/auth';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Alert, Spinner } from '../../components/UI';

const Login = () => {
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [loading,  setLoading]  = useState(false);
  const [error,    setError]    = useState('');
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res      = await axios.post('http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/user/login/', { username, password });
      const userData = res.data.user;
      saveTokens(res.data.access, res.data.refresh, userData);
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (userData.user_type === 'Admin')   navigate('/director/dashboard');
        if (userData.user_type === 'Student') navigate('/student/dashboard');
        if (userData.user_type === 'Teacher') navigate('/hod/staff/dashboard');
      }, 800);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Login failed:', err);
      setError(err.response?.data?.detail || 'Invalid credentials. Please try again.');
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="auth-wrapper">
      <div className="auth-container">
        <div className="auth-header">
          <h2>University Portal</h2>
          <p>Sign in to access your dashboard</p>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <Alert variant="error" mb="1rem">{error}</Alert>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <div className="form-group">
            <label>Phone / Email</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              className="form-input"
              value={username}
              onChange={e => setUsername(e.target.value)}
              required
              disabled={loading}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="form-group">
            <label>Password</label>
            <input
              type="password"
              className="form-input"
              value={password}
              onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)}
              required
              disabled={loading}
            />
          </div>
          <button
            type="submit"
            className="btn btn-primary"
            style={{ width: '100%', padding: '0.75rem', fontSize: '1rem', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: '.6rem' }}
            disabled={loading}
          >
            {loading ? (
              <>
                <Spinner size="18px" stroke="2px" />
                Signing in…
              </>
            ) : 'Sign In'}
          </button>
        </form>

        <div className="helper-text">
          <p><a href="#">Forgot your password?</a></p>
          <p>Contact IT Support for assistance.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default Login;

File: Department.jsx | Lines: 68
Path: client\src\page\director\Department.jsx

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import api from '../../services/api';
import { PageLoader } from '../../components/UI';
import { FaBuilding } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { MdArrowBack } from 'react-icons/md';

function Department() {
  const [departments, setDepartments] = useState([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  const navigate = useNavigate();

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchDepartments = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.get("structure/departments");
        setDepartments(res.data);
      } catch (err) {
        console.log(`Department error: ${err}`);
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false)
      }
    }
    fetchDepartments();
  }, []);
  return (
    <div>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '2rem' }}>
        <h2 style={{ margin: 0 }}><FaBuilding /> Department Overview</h2>
        <div onClick={() => { navigate(-1) }} style={{ display: "flex", cursor: "pointer" }}>
          <MdArrowBack size={24} />
          <span>Go back</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <PageLoader />
      ) : departments.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="data-grid">
          {departments.map((department) => (
            <div key={department.id} className="data-card">
              <div className="data-card-header"
                style={{
                  backgroundImage: `url(${department.logo})`,
                  backgroundSize: "cover",
                  backgroundPosition: "center",
                  backgroundRepeat: "no-repeat",
                }}>
                <h3 className="data-card-title">{department.name}</h3>
              </div>
              <div className="data-card-body">
                <p style={{ margin: 0 }}><strong>ID:</strong> {department.id}</p>
                <p style={{ marginTop: '0.5rem', marginBottom: 0, color: 'var(--accent)' }}>Click to view details</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ padding: '3rem', textAlign: 'center', background: 'white', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)' }}>
          <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>No department found in the system. Try synchronisation.</p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export default Department

const DirectorDashboard = () => {
  const [data,    setData]    = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const { isSyncing, syncError, syncSuccess, startSync } = useSync();
  const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{weekday:'long',year:'numeric',month:'long',day:'numeric'});

  useEffect(()=>{
    api.get('dashboard/director/dashboard')
      .then(r=>setData(r.data))
      .catch(console.error)
      .finally(()=>setLoading(false));
  },[]);

  if(loading) return <PageLoader label='Loading dashboard…' />;

  if(!data || data.error) return (
    <Page>
      <NoSchool>
        <h3>No school assigned</h3>
        <p>Your director account is not yet linked to a school. Please contact the system administrator.</p>
      </NoSchool>
    </Page>
  );

  const initials = data.school_name?.split(' ').map(w=>w[0]).join('').slice(0,2).toUpperCase();

  return (
    <Page>
      {/* School hero */}
      <SchoolHero>
        {data.school_logo
          ? <SchoolLogo src={data.school_logo} alt={data.school_name}/>
          : <SchoolLogoFallback>{initials}</SchoolLogoFallback>
        }
        <HeroInfo>
          <SchoolName>{data.school_name}</SchoolName>
          <SchoolCode>{data.school_code} · Director's Portal</SchoolCode>
          <p style={{margin:'.4rem 0 0',opacity:.65,fontSize:'.82rem'}}>{today}</p>
          <HeroActions>
            <SyncBtn onClick={startSync} disabled={isSyncing}>
              <SpinIcon $s={isSyncing}><FaSync size={12}/></SpinIcon>
              {isSyncing ? 'Synchronising…' : 'Synchronise Data'}
            </SyncBtn>
          </HeroActions>
        </HeroInfo>
      </SchoolHero>

      {/* Stats */}
      <SectionTitle>School Overview</SectionTitle>
      <StatsGrid>
        <StatCard $d=".05s">
          <IconBox $bg="#dcfce7" $c="#16a34a"><FaSitemap/></IconBox>
          <StatNum>{data.total_departments}</StatNum>
          <StatLbl>Departments</StatLbl>
        </StatCard>
        <StatCard $d=".10s">
          <IconBox $bg="#fce7f3" $c="#db2777"><FaChalkboardTeacher/></IconBox>
          <StatNum>{data.total_classes}</StatNum>
          <StatLbl>Classes</StatLbl>
        </StatCard>
        <StatCard $d=".15s">
          <IconBox $bg="#e0f2fe" $c="#0369a1"><FaUserGraduate/></IconBox>
          <StatNum>{data.total_students}</StatNum>
          <StatLbl>Students</StatLbl>
        </StatCard>
        <StatCard $d=".20s">
          <IconBox $bg="#fee2e2" $c="#dc2626"><FaExclamationTriangle/></IconBox>
          <AlertNum $warn={data.pending_complaints>0}>{data.pending_complaints}</AlertNum>
          <StatLbl>Pending Complaints</StatLbl>
        </StatCard>
        <StatCard $d=".25s">
          <IconBox $bg="#fef9c3" $c="#92400e"><FaFileAlt/></IconBox>
          <AlertNum $warn={data.pending_transcripts>0}>{data.pending_transcripts}</AlertNum>
          <StatLbl>Pending Transcripts</StatLbl>
        </StatCard>
        <StatCard $d=".30s">
          <IconBox $bg="#f3e8ff" $c="#7c3aed"><FaChartBar/></IconBox>
          <AlertNum $warn={data.unpublished_results>0}>{data.unpublished_results}</AlertNum>
          <StatLbl>Unpublished Results</StatLbl>
        </StatCard>
      </StatsGrid>

      {/* Departments in this school */}
      {data.departments?.length > 0 && (
        <>
          <SectionTitle>Departments in {data.school_name}</SectionTitle>
          <DeptGrid>
            {data.departments.map((d,i)=>(
              <DeptCard key={d.id} $d={`${i*0.05}s`}>
                <DeptTop $img={d.logo}>
                  <DeptName>{d.name}</DeptName>
                </DeptTop>
                <DeptBody>
                  <DeptMeta>
                    <div><strong>{d.classes}</strong> classes</div>
                    <div><strong>{d.students}</strong> students</div>
                  </DeptMeta>
                  <ViewBtn to="/director/department">
                    Details <FaArrowRight size={10}/>
                  </ViewBtn>
                </DeptBody>
              </DeptCard>
            ))}
          </DeptGrid>
        </>
      )}

      {/* Quick actions */}
      <SectionTitle>Quick Actions</SectionTitle>
      <QuickGrid>
        {[
          {to:'/director/department',    icon:<FaSitemap/>,           lbl:'Departments'},
          {to:'/director/level-degree',  icon:<FaChalkboardTeacher/>, lbl:'Level Degrees'},
          {to:'/hod/staff/transcripts',  icon:<FaFileAlt/>,           lbl:'Transcripts'},
          {to:'/announcements',          icon:<FaExclamationTriangle/>,lbl:'Announcements'},
          {to:'/timetables',             icon:<FaChartBar/>,          lbl:'Timetables'},
          {to:'/profile',                icon:<MdVerifiedUser/>,      lbl:'Profile & Signature'},
        ].map(q=>(
          <QuickCard key={q.to} to={q.to}>
            <span className="icon">{q.icon}</span>{q.lbl}
          </QuickCard>
        ))}
      </QuickGrid>
    </Page>
  );
};

export default DirectorDashboard;

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import api from '../../services/api';
import { PageLoader } from '../../components/UI';
import { FaGraduationCap } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { MdArrowBack } from 'react-icons/md';

const LevelDegree = () => {
  const [levelDegrees, setLevelDegrees] = useState([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  const navigate = useNavigate();

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchLevelDegrees = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.get("structure/level-degrees");
        setLevelDegrees(res.data);
      } catch (err) {
        console.log(`LevelDegree error: ${err}`);
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    }
    fetchLevelDegrees();
  }, []);
  return (
    <div>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '2rem' }}>
        <h2 style={{ margin: 0 }}><FaGraduationCap /> Level degree Overview</h2>
        <div onClick={() => { navigate(-1) }} style={{ display: "flex", cursor: "pointer" }}>
          <MdArrowBack size={24} />
          <span>Go back</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <PageLoader />
      ) : levelDegrees.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="data-grid">
          {levelDegrees.map((levelDegree) => (
            <div key={levelDegree.id} className="data-card">
              <div className="data-card-header">
                <h3 className="data-card-title">{levelDegree.name}</h3>
              </div>
              <div className="data-card-body">
                <p style={{ margin: 0 }}><strong>ID:</strong> {levelDegree.id}</p>
                <p style={{ marginTop: '0.5rem', marginBottom: 0, color: 'var(--accent)' }}>Click to view details</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ padding: '3rem', textAlign: 'center', background: 'white', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)' }}>
          <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>No level degree found in the system. Try synchronisation.</p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

import { PageLoader } from '../../components/UI';
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import api from "../../services/api"
import styled from 'styled-components'

const ClassGrid = styled.div`
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 1rem;
`;

const ClassItem = styled.div`
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 1rem;
  background: #f9fafb;
  border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
  border-radius: 6px;
`;
const Class = () => {
    const [classes,setClasses] = useState([]);
    const [isLoading,setIsLoading] = useState(true);
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    useEffect(()=>{
        const fetchClasses = async ()=>{
            try{
            const res = await api.get("structure/classes");
            setClasses(res.data);
            console.log(res);
            }catch(err){
                console.log(err);
            }finally{
                setIsLoading(false);
            }
        }
        fetchClasses();
    },[])

    const handleClick = (classId) =>{
        // navigate to detail page for selected class
        navigate(`/hod/staff/class/${classId}`);
    }
    return (

        <div>
            {isLoading ? 
            <PageLoader />:
            classes.length > 0 ?(
            <ClassGrid>
                <ClassItem style={{ background: 'var(--primary)', color: 'white', fontWeight: 'bold' }}>
                <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>Class Name</div>
                <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>Level</div>
                <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>Level Degree</div>
                <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>Number Enrolled</div>
                <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>Actions</div>
                </ClassItem>
                {classes.map((c) => (
                <ClassItem key={c.id}>
                    <div style={{ flex: 2 }}>{c.name}</div>
                    <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>{c.level}</div>
                    <div style={{ flex: 2 }}>{c.level_degree}</div>
                    <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>{c.num_students}</div>
                    <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                    <button style={{flex: 2}} 
                            onClick={()=>handleClick(c.id)} className="btn btn-primary">View Class details</button>
                    </div>
                </ClassItem>
                ))}
            </ClassGrid>
            ):(
            <p>No classes found for this staff.</p>
        )}
        </div>
    )
}
