import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import api from '../../services/api';
import { logOut } from '../../services/auth';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import PasswordReset from './PasswordReset';
import { RiLockPasswordLine } from 'react-icons/ri';
import { MdDateRange, MdEmail, MdPhone } from 'react-icons/md';
import { FaIdCard, FaUser, FaUserTag, FaVenusMars } from 'react-icons/fa';

const Profile = () => {
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState(null);
  const [signature, setSignature] = useState(null);
  const [sigFile, setSigFile] = useState(null);
  const [sigMessage, setSigMessage] = useState('');
  const [sigUploading, setSigUploading] = useState(false);

  const canUploadSignature = profile && ['Director', 'HOD', 'Staff'].includes(profile.role);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchProfile = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.get("user/profile/");
        setProfile(res.data);
      } catch (err) {
        console.log(`Profile error: ${err}`);
      }
    };
    fetchProfile();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (canUploadSignature) {
      api.get("user/signature/").then(res => {
        if (res.data.signature) setSignature(res.data.signature);
      }).catch(() => {});
    }
  }, [canUploadSignature]);

  const handleSignatureUpload = async () => {
    if (!sigFile) return;
    setSigUploading(true);
    setSigMessage('');
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('signature', sigFile);
    try {
      const res = await api.post("user/signature/", formData, { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'multipart/form-data' } });
      setSignature(res.data.signature);
      setSigMessage('Signature uploaded successfully.');
      setSigFile(null);
    } catch (err) {
      setSigMessage(err.response?.data?.error || 'Upload failed.');
    } finally {
      setSigUploading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
    {profile ?
    <div className="form-group">
        <div>
            <img src={profile.profile} alt="profile pic" style={{width:"7.1rem"}}/>
        </div>
      <div>
        <label htmlFor="name"><FaUser/> Name: </label>
        <input type="text" value={profile.fullname} className='form-input' disabled />
      </div>
      <div>
        <label htmlFor="matricule"><FaIdCard/> Matricule: </label>
        <input type="text" value={profile.matricule} className='form-input' disabled />
      </div>
      <div>
        <label htmlFor="smail"><MdEmail/> School-mail: </label>
        <input type="text" value={profile.email} className='form-input' disabled />
      </div>
      <div>
        <label htmlFor="phone"><MdPhone/> Phone: </label>
        <input type="text" value={profile.phone} className='form-input' disabled />
      </div>
      <div>
        <label htmlFor="role"><FaUserTag/> Role: </label>
        <input type="text" value={profile.role} className='form-input' disabled />
      </div>
      {profile.school &&
        <div>
        <label htmlFor="schoole"><FaVenusMars/> School: </label>
        <input type="text" value={profile.school} className='form-input' disabled />
      </div>
      }
      <div>
        <label htmlFor="dob"><MdDateRange/> Date Of Birth: </label>
        <input type="date" value={profile.dob} className='form-input' disabled />
      </div>
      <div>
        <label htmlFor="gender"><FaVenusMars/> Gender: </label>
        <input type="text" value={profile.gender} className='form-input' disabled />
      </div>
      

      {/* Digital Signature Section */}
      {canUploadSignature && (
        <div style={{ marginTop: '1.5rem', padding: '1.25rem', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)', borderRadius: '8px', background: 'var(--card-bg)' }}>
          <h4 style={{ margin: '0 0 1rem 0' }}>Digital Signature</h4>
          {signature ? (
            <div style={{ marginBottom: '1rem' }}>
              <p style={{ margin: '0 0 0.5rem 0', fontSize: '0.875rem', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>Current signature:</p>
              <img src={signature} alt="Digital signature" style={{ maxHeight: '80px', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)', borderRadius: '4px', padding: '0.5rem', background: 'white' }} />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', margin: '0 0 1rem 0' }}>No signature uploaded yet.</p>
          )}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '0.75rem', alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
            <input
              type="file"
              accept="image/*"
              onChange={e => setSigFile(e.target.files[0])}
              style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem' }}
            />
            <button
              className="btn btn-primary"
              onClick={handleSignatureUpload}
              disabled={!sigFile || sigUploading}
              style={{ padding: '0.4rem 1rem', fontSize: '0.875rem' }}
            >
              {sigUploading ? 'Uploading...' : signature ? 'Replace Signature' : 'Upload Signature'}
            </button>
          </div>
          {sigMessage && (
            <p style={{ marginTop: '0.5rem', fontSize: '0.875rem', color: sigMessage.includes('success') ? '#16a34a' : '#dc2626' }}>{sigMessage}</p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className='justify-space' style={{ marginTop: '1.5rem' }}>
        <button onClick={() => { logOut() }} className='btn btn-accent'>logout</button>
        <Link className='btn btn-primary' to="/update/password"> <RiLockPasswordLine/> change password</Link>
      </div>
    </div>
     : "Please login !!!!"
    }
    </>
  )
}

export default Profile

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import api from '../../services/api';
import { PageLoader } from '../../components/UI';
import styled, { keyframes } from 'styled-components';
import { FaPlus, FaFilePdf, FaDownload, FaTimes, FaExternalLinkAlt, FaCalendarAlt, FaBuilding } from 'react-icons/fa';

const fadeUp = keyframes`from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(14px)}to{opacity:1;transform:none}`;
const Page = styled.div`animation:${fadeUp} .4s ease both;`;

const Header = styled.div`display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:1.75rem;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:1rem;`;
const Title  = styled.h2`margin:0;font-size:1.6rem;`;
const CreateBtn = styled.button`
  display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.5rem;background:var(--primary);color:white;
  border:none;padding:.65rem 1.4rem;border-radius:8px;font-weight:600;font-size:.88rem;cursor:pointer;
  transition:opacity .2s;&:hover{opacity:.88;}
`;

const Grid = styled.div`display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(320px,1fr));gap:1.25rem;`;

const Card = styled.div`
  background:white;border:1px solid var(--border-color);border-radius:14px;overflow:hidden;
  box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.04);display:flex;flex-direction:column;
  animation:${fadeUp} .45s ease both;animation-delay:${p=>p.$d||'0s'};
  transition:transform .2s,box-shadow .2s;
  &:hover{transform:translateY(-2px);box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(30,58,138,.09);}
`;

const CardTop = styled.div`
  background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--primary),#1d4ed8);

`;

const MTitle  = styled.h4`margin:0;font-size:.95rem;`;
const MActions= styled.div`display:flex;gap:.5rem;align-items:center;`;
const CloseBtn= styled.button`background:none;border:1px solid var(--border-color);border-radius:6px;padding:.3rem .6rem;cursor:pointer;color:var(--text-secondary);display:flex;align-items:center;&:hover{background:#fee2e2;color:#dc2626;}`;
const PdfFrame= styled.iframe`flex:1;border:none;min-height:0;`;

const Empty = styled.div`text-align:center;padding:4rem 2rem;background:white;border-radius:14px;border:1px solid var(--border-color);color:var(--text-secondary);`;

const BASE = 'http://127.0.0.1:8000';
function resolveUrl(u){ return u?.startsWith('http') ? u : `${BASE}${u}`; }

const Timetables = () => {
  const [items,   setItems]   = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [user,    setUser]    = useState(null);
  const [modal,   setModal]   = useState(null);

  useEffect(()=>{
    setUser(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('user')));
    api.get('announcements/timetables')
      .then(r=>setItems(r.data))
      .catch(console.error)
      .finally(()=>setLoading(false));
  },[]);

  const download = async (url, name) => {
    const full = resolveUrl(url);
    const res  = await fetch(full);
    const blob = await res.blob();
    const a    = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob); a.download = name||'timetable.pdf';
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
  };

  const canCreate = user?.user_type==='Teacher';

  if(loading) return <PageLoader label='Loading timetables…' />;

  return (
    <Page>
      <Header>
        <Title>Timetables</Title>
        {canCreate && <CreateBtn onClick={()=>window.location.href='/create-timetable'}><FaPlus/> Upload Timetable</CreateBtn>}
      </Header>

      {items.length===0 ? (
        <Empty>
          <FaFilePdf size={40} style={{opacity:.3,marginBottom:'1rem',color:'#dc2626'}}/>
          <h3>No timetables available</h3>
          <p>Check back later for updates.</p>
        </Empty>
      ) : (
        <Grid>
          {items.map((t,i)=>{
            const pdfUrl  = resolveUrl(t.pdf_file);
            const pdfName = `${t.title}.pdf`;
            return (
              <Card key={t.id} $d={`${Math.min(i,5)*0.05}s`}>
                <CardTop>
                  <div>
                    <TTitle>{t.title}</TTitle>
                    <div style={{opacity:.75,fontSize:'.78rem'}}>{new Date(t.uploaded_at).toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{year:'numeric',month:'short',day:'numeric'})}</div>
                  </div>
                  <DeptBadge>{t.department_name}</DeptBadge>
                </CardTop>
                <CardBody>
                  <MetaRow><FaBuilding size={11}/> {t.department_name}</MetaRow>
                  <MetaRow><FaCalendarAlt size={11}/> Uploaded by {t.uploaded_by_name||'—'}</MetaRow>
                  {t.classes?.length > 0 && (
                    <div>
                      <div style={{fontSize:'.75rem',fontWeight:700,color:'var(--text-secondary)',textTransform:'uppercase',letterSpacing:'.04em',marginBottom:'.35rem'}}>Classes</div>
                      <ClassTags>
                        {t.classes.map(c=><ClassTag key={c.id}>{c.class_name}</ClassTag>)}
                      </ClassTags>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </CardBody>
                <CardFooter>
                  <Btn $primary onClick={()=>setModal({url:pdfUrl,name:pdfName})}>
                    <FaExternalLinkAlt size={10}/> View PDF
                  </Btn>
                  <Btn onClick={()=>download(t.pdf_file, pdfName)}>
                    <FaDownload size={10}/> Download
                  </Btn>
                </CardFooter>
              </Card>
            );
          })}
        </Grid>
      )}

      {/* PDF Modal */}
      {modal && (
        <Overlay onClick={()=>setModal(null)}>
          <Modal onClick={e=>e.stopPropagation()}>
            <MHeader>
              <MTitle><FaFilePdf style={{marginRight:'.4rem',color:'#dc2626'}}/>{modal.name}</MTitle>
              <MActions>
                <Btn style={{flex:'none',padding:'.35rem .8rem',fontSize:'.78rem'}} onClick={()=>download(modal.url,modal.name)}>
                  <FaDownload size={11}/> Download
                </Btn>
                <CloseBtn onClick={()=>setModal(null)}><FaTimes size={14}/></CloseBtn>
              </MActions>
            </MHeader>
            <PdfFrame src={modal.url} title={modal.name}/>
          </Modal>
        </Overlay>
      )}
    </Page>
  );
};

export default Timetables;

import axios from "axios";
import { getAccessToken,refreshToken,clearTokens } from "./auth";

const api = axios.create({
    baseURL: "http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/",
})


api.interceptors.request.use(
    async (config) =>{
        const token = getAccessToken()
        if(token){
            config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
        }

        return config;
    },
    (error) => Promise.reject(error)
);

// Handle 401 errors

let isRefreshing = false;
let refreshSubscribers = [];

function onRefreshed(token) {
  refreshSubscribers.forEach((callback) => callback(token));
  refreshSubscribers = [];
}

function subscribeTokenRefresh(cb) {
  refreshSubscribers.push(cb);
}

api.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  async (error) => {
    const originalRequest = error.config;

    // if 401 and no refresh attempts
    if (error.response && error.response.status === 401 && !originalRequest._retry) {
      if (isRefreshing) {
        // Wait till refresh ends
        return new Promise((resolve) => {
          subscribeTokenRefresh((token) => {
            originalRequest.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
            resolve(api(originalRequest));
          });
        });
      }

      originalRequest._retry = true;
      isRefreshing = true;

      const newToken = await refreshToken();
      isRefreshing = false;

      if (newToken) {
        onRefreshed(newToken);
        originalRequest.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${newToken}`;
        return api(originalRequest);
      } else {
        clearTokens();
        window.location.href = "/login";
      }
    }

    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);

export default api;

------------------------------------------------------------
File: auth.js | Lines: 43
Path: client\src\services\auth.js
------------------------------------------------------------
import api from "./api";

export const getAccessToken = () => localStorage.getItem("access");
export const getRefreshToken = () => localStorage.getItem("refresh");


export const saveTokens = (access, refresh, user) => {
    if (access) localStorage.setItem("access", access)
    if (refresh) localStorage.setItem("refresh", refresh)
    if (user) localStorage.setItem("user", JSON.stringify(user))
};

export const clearTokens = () => {
    localStorage.removeItem("access");
    localStorage.removeItem("refresh");
    localStorage.removeItem("user");
};

export const logOut = () => {
    clearTokens();
    window.location.href = "/login";
}

export const refreshToken = async () => {
    const refresh = getRefreshToken()

    if (!refresh) return null;

    try {
        const response = await api.post("/user/refresh-token/", { refresh });
        const newAccess = response.data.access;
        saveTokens(newAccess, refresh)
        console.log("new token gotten ")
        return newAccess;
    } catch (err) {
        console.warn(`Refresh invalide or Expired ${err}`)

        clearTokens();
        return null;
    }
}
